5.3 Pattern: Orchestrated Workflow
Когда использовать: Multi-step business processes with dependencies
Orchestrator coordinates sequence:
Step 1 → Service A → wait for completion
Step 2 → Service B → wait for completion
Step 3 → Service C → wait for completion

Example: ISO 22301 Implementation (12 months)
Temporal.io Workflow:
python# workflows/iso22301_implementation.py

from temporalio import workflow
from datetime import timedelta

@workflow.defn
class ISO22301ImplementationWorkflow:
    @workflow.run
    async def run(self, org_id: str) -> dict:
        """
        12-month ISO 22301 certification journey
        """
        
        # Phase 1: Context & Planning (Month 1-2)
        await workflow.execute_activity(
            create_context_analysis,
            args=[org_id],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(hours=24)
        )
        
        await workflow.execute_activity(
            set_bc_objectives,
            args=[org_id],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(hours=48)
        )
        
        # Phase 2: Analysis (Month 3-4)
        bia_result = await workflow.execute_activity(
            conduct_bia,
            args=[org_id],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=30)
        )
        
        # Wait for BIA approval from user
        await workflow.wait_condition(
            lambda: self.bia_approved,
            timeout=timedelta(days=14)
        )
        
        risk_result = await workflow.execute_activity(
            assess_risks,
            args=[org_id, bia_result],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=30)
        )
        
        # Phase 3: Strategy & Plans (Month 5-7)
        strategies = await workflow.execute_activity(
            develop_strategies,
            args=[org_id, bia_result, risk_result],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=30)
        )
        
        # Wait for strategy selection
        await workflow.wait_condition(
            lambda: self.strategy_selected,
            timeout=timedelta(days=14)
        )
        
        plans = await workflow.execute_activity(
            create_bc_plans,
            args=[org_id, self.selected_strategy],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=60)
        )
        
        # Phase 4: Implementation (Month 8-9)
        await workflow.execute_activity(
            train_staff,
            args=[org_id],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=30)
        )
        
        # Phase 5: Testing (Month 10)
        exercise_results = await workflow.execute_activity(
            conduct_exercises,
            args=[org_id, plans],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=30)
        )
        
        # Fix issues from exercises
        if exercise_results.issues:
            await workflow.execute_activity(
                implement_corrective_actions,
                args=[org_id, exercise_results.issues],
                start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=30)
            )
        
        # Phase 6: Audit Preparation (Month 11)
        await workflow.execute_activity(
            conduct_internal_audit,
            args=[org_id],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=14)
        )
        
        await workflow.execute_activity(
            management_review,
            args=[org_id],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=7)
        )
        
        # Phase 7: Certification (Month 12)
        readiness = await workflow.execute_activity(
            assess_certification_readiness,
            args=[org_id],
            start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=7)
        )
        
        if readiness.ready:
            certification = await workflow.execute_activity(
                schedule_certification_audit,
                args=[org_id],
                start_to_close_timeout=timedelta(days=90)
            )
            
            return {
                "status": "completed",
                "certification": certification,
                "duration_months": 12
            }
        else:
            return {
                "status": "not_ready",
                "gaps": readiness.gaps,
                "recommended_actions": readiness.actions
            }
5.4 Pattern: Saga (Distributed Transaction)
Когда использовать: Multi-service transaction with rollback capability
Example: Create Organization with Full Setup

Success flow:
1. Create Org → SUCCESS
2. Create BIA template → SUCCESS
3. Create default policies → SUCCESS
4. Assign admin user → SUCCESS
✅ Commit all

Failure flow:
1. Create Org → SUCCESS
2. Create BIA template → SUCCESS
3. Create default policies → FAILURE
❌ Rollback:
   - Delete BIA template
   - Delete Org
Implementation:
python# saga_orchestrator.py

class CreateOrganizationSaga:
    def __init__(self):
        self.completed_steps = []
    
    async def execute(self, org_data: dict):
        try:
            # Step 1: Create Organization
            org_id = await self._create_organization(org_data)
            self.completed_steps.append(("create_org", org_id))
            
            # Step 2: Create BIA template
            bia_id = await self._create_bia_template(org_id)
            self.completed_steps.append(("create_bia", bia_id))
            
            # Step 3: Create default policies
            policies = await self._create_default_policies(org_id)
            self.completed_steps.append(("create_policies", policies))
            
            # Step 4: Assign admin
            await self._assign_admin_user(org_id, org_data["admin_email"])
            self.completed_steps.append(("assign_admin", None))
            
            return {"status": "success", "org_id": org_id}
            
        except Exception as e:
            # Rollback
            await self._rollback()
            raise
    
    async def _rollback(self):
        """Execute compensating transactions in reverse order"""
        for step_name, step_data in reversed(self.completed_steps):
            if step_name == "create_org":
                await governance_service.delete_organization(step_data)
            elif step_name == "create_bia":
                await bia_service.delete_bia(step_data)
            elif step_name == "create_policies":
                await documents_service.delete_policies(step_data)
            elif step_name == "assign_admin":
                await auth_service.revoke_admin(step_data)

6. DEPLOYMENT ARCHITECTURE
6.1 Development Environment
localhost development:
- Each service runs as separate Python process
- Shared PostgreSQL (localhost:5432)
- Shared Redis (localhost:6379)
- Neo4j (localhost:7687)
- MinIO (localhost:9000)

Start all services:
$ python orchestrator/main.py &      # :8002
$ python eventbus/main.py &          # :8001
$ python api_gateway/main.py &       # :8000
$ python services/governance/main.py & # :8010
$ python services/bia/main.py &      # :8011
$ python services/risk/main.py &     # :8013
...
6.2 Docker Compose (staging/local prod)
yaml# docker-compose.yml

version: '3.8'

services:
  # Infrastructure
  postgres:
    image: postgres:15-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: bcm_platform
      POSTGRES_USER: bcm
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
  
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
    volumes:
      - redis_data:/data
  
  neo4j:
    image: neo4j:5-community
    environment:
      NEO4J_AUTH: neo4j/${NEO4J_PASSWORD}
    ports:
      - "7474:7474"  # HTTP
      - "7687:7687"  # Bolt
    volumes:
      - neo4j_data:/data
  
  minio:
    image: minio/minio:latest
    command: server /data --console-address ":9001"
    environment:
      MINIO_ROOT_USER: ${MINIO_USER}
      MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD: ${MINIO_PASSWORD}
    ports:
      - "9000:9000"  # API
      - "9001:9001"  # Console
    volumes:
      - minio_data:/data
  
  # Core Services
  api-gateway:
    build: ./api_gateway
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379
      JWT_SECRET: ${JWT_SECRET}
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - redis
  
  orchestrator:
    build: ./orchestrator
    ports:
      - "8002:8002"
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - redis
  
  eventbus:
    build: ./eventbus
    ports:
      - "8001:8001"
    environment:
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379
    depends_on:
      - redis
  
  # BCM Services
  governance:
    build: ./services/governance
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      SERVICE_PORT: 8010
      EVENTBUS_URL: http://eventbus:8001
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - eventbus
  
  bia:
    build: ./services/bia
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      SERVICE_PORT: 8011
      EVENTBUS_URL: http://eventbus:8001
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - eventbus
  
  risk:
    build: ./services/risk
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      SERVICE_PORT: 8013
      EVENTBUS_URL: http://eventbus:8001
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - eventbus
  
  planning:
    build: ./services/planning
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      SERVICE_PORT: 8015
      EVENTBUS_URL: http://eventbus:8001
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - eventbus
  
  # ... other services
  
  # AI Services
  mcp-bcm-expert:
    build: ./mcp_servers/bcm_expert
    environment:
      PLATFORM_API_URL: http://api-gateway:8000
      OPENAI_API_KEY: ${OPENAI_API_KEY}
      ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${ANTHROPIC_API_KEY}
      PINECONE_API_KEY: ${PINECONE_API_KEY}
    depends_on:
      - api-gateway
  
  # Frontend
  web-app:
    build: ./frontend
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    environment:
      NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL: http://api-gateway:8000
    depends_on:
      - api-gateway
  
  # Monitoring
  prometheus:
    image: prom/prometheus:latest
    ports:
      - "9090:9090"
    volumes:
      - ./monitoring/prometheus.yml:/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml
  
  grafana:
    image: grafana/grafana:latest
    ports:
      - "3001:3000"
    environment:
      GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD: ${GRAFANA_PASSWORD}
    depends_on:
      - prometheus

volumes:
  postgres_data:
  redis_data:
  neo4j_data:
  minio_data:
6.3 Kubernetes (production)
yaml# k8s/namespace.yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Namespace
metadata:
  name: bcm-platform

---
# k8s/api-gateway-deployment.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: api-gateway
  namespace: bcm-platform
spec:
  replicas: 3
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: api-gateway
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: api-gateway
    spec:
      containers:
      - name: api-gateway
        image: bcm-platform/api-gateway:1.0.0
        ports:
        - containerPort: 8000
        env:
        - name: DATABASE_URL
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: db-credentials
              key: url
        - name: REDIS_URL
          value: redis://redis-service:6379
        resources:
          requests:
            memory: "256Mi"
            cpu: "250m"
          limits:
            memory: "512Mi"
            cpu: "500m"
        livenessProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /health
            port: 8000
          initialDelaySeconds: 30
          periodSeconds: 10
        readinessProbe:
          httpGet:
            path: /ready
            port: 8000
          initialDelaySeconds: 5
          periodSeconds: 5

---
# k8s/api-gateway-service.yaml
apiVersion: v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: api-gateway
  namespace: bcm-platform
spec:
  type: LoadBalancer
  selector:
    app: api-gateway
  ports:
  - port: 80
    targetPort: 8000

---
# k8s/bia-service-deployment.yaml
apiVersion: apps/v1
kind: Deployment
metadata:
  name: bia-service
  namespace: bcm-platform
spec:
  replicas: 2
  selector:
    matchLabels:
      app: bia-service
  template:
    metadata:
      labels:
        app: bia-service
    spec:
      containers:
      - name: bia-service
        image: bcm-platform/bia-service:1.0.0
        ports:
        - containerPort: 8011
        env:
        - name: DATABASE_URL
          valueFrom:
            secretKeyRef:
              name: db-credentials
              key: url
        - name: SERVICE_PORT
          value: "8011"
        - name: EVENTBUS_URL
          value: http://eventbus-service:8001

---
# k8s/ingress.yaml
apiVersion: networking.k8s.io/v1
kind: Ingress
metadata:
  name: bcm-platform-ingress
  namespace: bcm-platform
  annotations:
    cert-manager.io/cluster-issuer: letsencrypt-prod
    nginx.ingress.kubernetes.io/ssl-redirect: "true"
spec:
  ingressClassName: nginx
  tls:
  - hosts:
    - bcm-platform.com
    - api.bcm-platform.com
    secretName: bcm-platform-tls
  rules:
  - host: bcm-platform.com
    http:
      paths:
      - path: /
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: web-app
            port:
              number: 3000
  - host: api.bcm-platform.com
    http:
      paths:
      - path: /
        pathType: Prefix
        backend:
          service:
            name: api-gateway
            port:
              number: 80

7. DATA FLOW DIAGRAMS
7.1 User Journey: Conduct BIA
USER → Frontend → API Gateway → BIA Service → Database
                                     ↓
                                 EventBus
                                     ↓
                    ┌────────────────┼────────────────┐
                    ▼                ▼                ▼
              Risk Service    Planning Service   Compliance
                    ↓                ↓                ↓
                Database        Database        Database
Detailed Flow:

User opens BIA module

   GET /api/bia/{org_id}/current
   → API Gateway → BIA Service → PostgreSQL
   ← Returns existing BIA or null

User clicks "Start New BIA"

   POST /api/bia/start
   body: { org_id: "hospital_001" }
   
   → API Gateway (validates JWT)
   → BIA Service
     → Creates BIA record in DB
     → Returns bia_id
     → Publishes event "bia.started"

User adds business process

   POST /api/bia/{bia_id}/processes
   body: {
     name: "Emergency Department",
     description: "24/7 emergency care",
     owner: "Dr. Smith"
   }
   
   → BIA Service
     → Saves process
     → Returns process_id

User analyzes impact (may use AI)

   POST /api/bia/{bia_id}/analyze/{process_id}
   
   → BIA Service
     → Calls AI Expert (MCP or embedded)
     → AI analyzes: financial/operational/reputational impact
     → Saves impact assessment
     → Publishes "bia.process.analyzed"

User determines RTO (AI recommendation)

   POST /api/bia/{bia_id}/rto/{process_id}
   
   → BIA Service
     → Calls AI Expert: recommend_rto
     → AI returns: RTO=0h, rationale="Tier 1 critical"
     → Saves RTO
     → Updates process record

User completes BIA

   POST /api/bia/{bia_id}/complete
   
   → BIA Service
     → Validates all processes analyzed
     → Sets status = "completed"
     → Publishes "bia.completed" with full results
   
   → EventBus broadcasts to:
      - Risk Service (starts risk assessment)
      - Planning Service (prepares strategies)
      - Compliance Service (checks ISO 8.2.2)
      - Digital Twin (updates model)

Frontend shows results + next steps

   WebSocket message received:
   {
     type: "bia.completed",
     data: {
       critical_processes: 8,
       rto_requirements: {...},
       next_actions: [
         "Start Risk Assessment",
         "Review BC Strategies"
       ]
     }
   }
   
   UI updates in real-time
7.2 AI Expert Flow (MCP)
Claude Desktop → MCP Client → BCM Expert MCP Server → Platform API
                                      ↓
                                  LLM + RAG
                                      ↓
                                  Response
                                      ↓
                                Platform saves data
Example: User asks Claude for RTO recommendation
User in Claude Desktop:
"Recommend RTO for Emergency Department in our hospital. 
We handle 200 patients/day."

Claude (via MCP):
1. Detects need for bcm_expert.recommend_rto tool
2. Calls MCP server:
   
   recommend_rto(
     org_id="hospital_001",  # from context
     process_name="Emergency Department"
   )

3. MCP Server:
   - Fetches org context from Platform API
   - Fetches existing BIA data
   - RAG retrieves ISO 22301 + healthcare guidelines
   - LLM analyzes and recommends
   - Saves RTO back to Platform
   - Returns recommendation

4. Claude shows user:
   "For Emergency Department, I recommend RTO = 0 hours (immediate).
   
   Rationale:
   - Tier 1 critical service per CMS requirements
   - 200 patients/day = $50K/hour downtime cost
   - Patient safety requires immediate availability
   
   ⚠️ I've identified gaps:
   - EMR system current RTO: 4 hours (needs: 15 min)
   - Lab services current RTO: 2 hours (needs: 30 min)
   
   View full BIA: https://bcm-platform.com/bia/hospital_001"
Data saved to platform:
json{
  "process_id": "ed_001",
  "rto_hours": 0,
  "rto_rationale": "...",
  "ai_recommendation": true,
  "recommended_at": "2025-10-02T10:30:00Z",
  "gaps_identified": [...]
}

8. SECURITY ARCHITECTURE
8.1 Authentication & Authorization
┌──────────────┐
│    User      │
└──────┬───────┘
       │ login
       ▼
┌──────────────┐
│ API Gateway  │ JWT generation
└──────┬───────┘
       │ JWT token
       ▼
┌──────────────┐
│  Frontend    │ stores token
└──────┬───────┘
       │ every request includes JWT
       ▼
┌──────────────┐
│ API Gateway  │ validates JWT
└──────┬───────┘
       │ forwards with user context
       ▼
┌──────────────┐
│   Services   │ check permissions
└──────────────┘
JWT Structure:
json{
  "sub": "user_123",
  "org_id": "hospital_001",
  "roles": ["bcm_manager", "admin"],
  "permissions": [
    "bia:read",
    "bia:write",
    "risk:read",
    "plan:write"
  ],
  "exp": 1733145600
}
RBAC (Role-Based Access Control):
python# Roles
roles = {
    "admin": ["*"],  # full access
    "bcm_manager": [
        "bia:*", "risk:*", "plan:*",
        "compliance:read", "doc:*"
    ],
    "consultant": [
        "bia:read", "risk:read", "plan:read",
        "compliance:read", "doc:read"
    ],
    "auditor": [
        "compliance:*", "doc:read", "audit:*"
    ],
    "viewer": [
        "*.read"
    ]
}
8.2 Data Encryption
At Rest:

PostgreSQL: Transparent Data Encryption (TDE)
MinIO: Server-side encryption (SSE)
Secrets: Vault/AWS Secrets Manager

In Transit:

TLS 1.3 for all HTTP traffic
Certificate from Let's Encrypt (auto-renewal)

8.3 Audit Trail
python# Every data mutation logged
@app.post("/organizations")
async def create_organization(org: Org, user: User):
    org_id = await repo.create(org)
    
    # Audit log
    await audit.log(
        action="organization.created",
        user_id=user.id,
        resource_type="organization",
        resource_id=org_id,
        changes=org.dict(),
        ip_address=request.client.host,
        timestamp=datetime.now()
    )
    
    return {"id": org_id}
Audit log stored in immutable append-only table:
sqlCREATE TABLE audit_log (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,
    timestamp TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,
    user_id VARCHAR NOT NULL,
    action VARCHAR NOT NULL,
    resource_type VARCHAR NOT NULL,
    resource_id VARCHAR NOT NULL,
    changes JSONB,
    ip_address INET,
    -- Immutable
    CONSTRAINT audit_no_update CHECK (false)
);

-- Prevent updates/deletes
CREATE RULE audit_no_update AS ON UPDATE TO audit_log DO INSTEAD NOTHING;
CREATE RULE audit_no_delete AS ON DELETE TO audit_log DO INSTEAD NOTHING;

9. MONITORING & OBSERVABILITY
9.1 Metrics (Prometheus)
python# Each service exposes metrics

from prometheus_client import Counter, Histogram, Gauge

# Request metrics
http_requests_total = Counter(
    'http_requests_total',
    'Total HTTP requests',
    ['method', 'endpoint', 'status']
)

http_request_duration = Histogram(
    'http_request_duration_seconds',
    'HTTP request duration',
    ['method', 'endpoint']
)

# Business metrics
bia_completed = Counter(
    'bia_completed_total',
    'Total BIAs completed'
)

bia_duration = Histogram(
    'bia_duration_days',
    'BIA completion time in days'
)

# System health
service_health = Gauge(
    'service_health',
    'Service health status',
    ['service_name']
)

pythonimport structlog

logger = structlog.get_logger()

# Every log structured
logger.info(
    "bia_completed",
    org_id="hospital_001",
    bia_id="bia_123",
    duration_days=14,
    critical_processes=8,
    user_id="user_456"
)
9.3 Tracing (Distributed)
python# OpenTelemetry for distributed tracing

from opentelemetry import trace

tracer = trace.get_tracer(__name__)

@app.post("/bia/{bia_id}/complete")
async def complete_bia(bia_id: str):
    with tracer.start_as_current_span("complete_bia") as span:
        span.set_attribute("bia_id", bia_id)
        
        # Trace propagates through services
        results = await bia_service.complete(bia_id)
        
        span.set_attribute("processes_count", len(results.processes))
        return results

│  │  └─ llm.generate [0.7s]
│  └─ database.save [0.2s]
└─ eventbus.publish [0.5s]
   ├─ risk_service.on_bia_completed [0.3s]
   └─ planning_service.on_bia_completed [0.2s]

Я увлёкся общей архитектурой и забыл про исходную проблему: как AI Advisor понимает где пользователь находится в workflow и что ему предлагать дальше.
Давай вернёмся к этому:

AI Advisor + Workflow State Machine
Проблема
У тебя есть:

Модули с workflow (BIA: шаг 1 → шаг 2 → шаг 3...)
AI коллега который должен помогать пользователю
Вопрос: Как AI знает где пользователь сейчас в workflow и что предложить?

Решение: State Machine + Context-Aware AI
Workflow State ──┐
                 ├──> AI Advisor получает контекст
User Context ────┤
                 │    ↓
Module Rules ────┘    AI понимает:
                      - Где мы сейчас?
                      - Что уже сделано?
                      - Что дальше?
                      - Какие проблемы?

Конкретная реализация для BIA модуля
1. Workflow State Machine (в коде платформы)
python# modules/bia/workflow.py

from enum import Enum
from typing import List, Optional

class BIAStage(Enum):
    """Стадии BIA workflow"""
    NOT_STARTED = "not_started"
    IDENTIFY_PROCESSES = "identify_processes"
    ANALYZE_DEPENDENCIES = "analyze_dependencies"
    ASSESS_IMPACT = "assess_impact"
    DETERMINE_RTO = "determine_rto"
    REVIEW_RESULTS = "review_results"
    COMPLETED = "completed"

class BIAWorkflow:
    """State machine для BIA процесса"""
    
    # Правила перехода между стадиями
    TRANSITIONS = {
        BIAStage.NOT_STARTED: [BIAStage.IDENTIFY_PROCESSES],
        BIAStage.IDENTIFY_PROCESSES: [BIAStage.ANALYZE_DEPENDENCIES],
        BIAStage.ANALYZE_DEPENDENCIES: [BIAStage.ASSESS_IMPACT],
        BIAStage.ASSESS_IMPACT: [BIAStage.DETERMINE_RTO],
        BIAStage.DETERMINE_RTO: [BIAStage.REVIEW_RESULTS],
        BIAStage.REVIEW_RESULTS: [
            BIAStage.COMPLETED,
            BIAStage.IDENTIFY_PROCESSES  # можно вернуться добавить процессы
        ],
        BIAStage.COMPLETED: []
    }
    
    # Что нужно для каждой стадии
    REQUIREMENTS = {
        BIAStage.IDENTIFY_PROCESSES: {
            "min_processes": 3,
            "required_fields": ["name", "description", "owner"]
        },
        BIAStage.ANALYZE_DEPENDENCIES: {
            "dependencies_per_process": 2,
            "dependency_types": ["people", "tech", "facility", "supplier"]
        },
        BIAStage.ASSESS_IMPACT: {
            "impact_types": ["financial", "operational", "reputational", "regulatory"]
        },
        BIAStage.DETERMINE_RTO: {
            "required_fields": ["rto", "rpo", "mtpd", "rationale"]
        }
    }
    
    def __init__(self, bia_id: str):
        self.bia_id = bia_id
        self.current_stage = BIAStage.NOT_STARTED
        self.completed_steps = []
        self.data = {}
    
    async def get_current_context(self) -> dict:
        """
        Получить полный контекст для AI Advisor
        """
        # Загрузить данные из БД
        bia_data = await db.get_bia(self.bia_id)
        
        return {
            "bia_id": self.bia_id,
            "current_stage": self.current_stage.value,
            "current_stage_label": self._stage_label(self.current_stage),
            "progress_percentage": self._calculate_progress(),
            "completed_steps": self.completed_steps,
            
            # Данные по текущей стадии
            "current_stage_data": {
                "processes_count": len(bia_data.processes),
                "processes": [p.dict() for p in bia_data.processes],
                "last_updated": bia_data.updated_at
            },
            
            # Что не хватает для следующего шага
            "gaps": self._identify_gaps(bia_data),
            
            # Что можно делать дальше
            "available_actions": self._get_available_actions(),
            
            # Проблемы/предупреждения
            "issues": self._identify_issues(bia_data)
        }
    
    def _identify_gaps(self, bia_data) -> List[dict]:
        """Что не хватает для завершения текущей стадии"""
        gaps = []
        requirements = self.REQUIREMENTS.get(self.current_stage, {})
        
        if self.current_stage == BIAStage.IDENTIFY_PROCESSES:
            min_processes = requirements.get("min_processes", 3)
            if len(bia_data.processes) < min_processes:
                gaps.append({
                    "type": "insufficient_data",
                    "field": "processes",
                    "message": f"Need at least {min_processes} processes, have {len(bia_data.processes)}",
                    "severity": "critical"
                })
            
            for process in bia_data.processes:
                for field in requirements["required_fields"]:
                    if not getattr(process, field):
                        gaps.append({
                            "type": "missing_field",
                            "process": process.name,
                            "field": field,
                            "message": f"Process '{process.name}' missing {field}",
                            "severity": "high"
                        })
        
        elif self.current_stage == BIAStage.ANALYZE_DEPENDENCIES:
            for process in bia_data.processes:
                if len(process.dependencies) < requirements["dependencies_per_process"]:
                    gaps.append({
                        "type": "insufficient_dependencies",
                        "process": process.name,
                        "message": f"Process '{process.name}' needs more dependencies",
                        "severity": "medium"
                    })
        
        return gaps
    
    def _get_available_actions(self) -> List[dict]:
        """Что пользователь может делать сейчас"""
        actions = []
        
        if self.current_stage == BIAStage.IDENTIFY_PROCESSES:
            actions.append({
                "id": "add_process",
                "label": "Add Business Process",
                "type": "primary",
                "requires_ai": False
            })
            actions.append({
                "id": "ai_suggest_processes",
                "label": "AI: Suggest Processes for My Industry",
                "type": "secondary",
                "requires_ai": True
            })
            
            if len(self.data.get("processes", [])) >= 3:
                actions.append({
                    "id": "move_to_dependencies",
                    "label": "Next: Analyze Dependencies",
                    "type": "primary",
                    "requires_ai": False
                })
        
        elif self.current_stage == BIAStage.ASSESS_IMPACT:
            actions.append({
                "id": "ai_analyze_impact",
                "label": "AI: Analyze Impact for Selected Process",
                "type": "primary",
                "requires_ai": True
            })
        
        return actions
    
    async def transition_to(self, next_stage: BIAStage) -> bool:
        """Переход на следующую стадию (с валидацией)"""
        if next_stage not in self.TRANSITIONS[self.current_stage]:
            return False
        
        # Проверить что текущая стадия завершена
        if not await self._stage_completed(self.current_stage):
            return False
        
        self.current_stage = next_stage
        self.completed_steps.append({
            "stage": next_stage.value,
            "completed_at": datetime.now()
        })
        
        await db.update_bia_stage(self.bia_id, next_stage.value)
        
        # Уведомить AI Advisor о смене стадии
        await eventbus.publish("bia.stage.changed", {
            "bia_id": self.bia_id,
            "new_stage": next_stage.value
        })
        
        return True

2. AI Advisor получает контекст workflow
python# ai/advisor.py

class BIAAdvisor:
    """AI коллега для BIA модуля"""
    
    async def get_contextual_advice(
        self,
        bia_id: str,
        user_message: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Даёт советы на основе текущего состояния workflow
        """
        
        # 1. Получить контекст workflow
        workflow = BIAWorkflow(bia_id)
        context = await workflow.get_current_context()
        
        # 2. Построить prompt для AI
        prompt = f"""
You are a BCM expert helping a user conduct Business Impact Analysis (BIA).

CURRENT CONTEXT:
- BIA Stage: {context['current_stage_label']}
- Progress: {context['progress_percentage']}%
- Processes identified: {context['current_stage_data']['processes_count']}

GAPS (what's missing):
{json.dumps(context['gaps'], indent=2)}

AVAILABLE ACTIONS:
{json.dumps(context['available_actions'], indent=2)}

ISSUES:
{json.dumps(context['issues'], indent=2)}

USER MESSAGE (if any): {user_message or "None - proactive guidance requested"}

YOUR TASK:
1. Analyze current situation
2. Provide specific, actionable advice
3. If gaps exist, explain how to fix them
4. Suggest next concrete steps
5. If user asked a question, answer it in context

Be conversational, encouraging, and specific. Don't repeat what they already know.
"""
        
        # 3. Вызвать LLM
        response = await llm_generate(
            prompt=prompt,
            model="claude-sonnet-4",
            temperature=0.7
        )
        
        # 4. Вернуть структурированный ответ
        return {
            "message": response.text,
            "suggested_actions": self._extract_actions(response),
            "context": context,
            "proactive": user_message is None
        }
    
    async def handle_user_action(
        self,
        bia_id: str,
        action_id: str,
        params: dict
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Выполнить действие которое запросил пользователь
        """
        
        if action_id == "ai_suggest_processes":
            return await self._suggest_processes(bia_id, params)
        
        elif action_id == "ai_analyze_impact":
            return await self._analyze_impact(bia_id, params)
        
        elif action_id == "ai_recommend_rto":
            return await self._recommend_rto(bia_id, params)
    
    async def _suggest_processes(
        self,
        bia_id: str,
        params: dict
    ) -> dict:
        """AI предлагает типичные процессы для индустрии"""
        
        org = await db.get_organization(bia_id)
        
        prompt = f"""
Based on ISO 22301 and BCI Good Practice Guidelines:

Suggest 10-15 typical business processes for:
Industry: {org.industry}
Size: {org.size}
Type: {org.org_type}

For each process provide:
- Name
- Brief description
- Typical criticality tier (1-4)
- Common dependencies

Format as JSON array.
"""
        
        suggestions = await llm_generate(
            prompt=prompt,
            structured_output=ProcessSuggestionsSchema
        )
        
        return {
            "suggested_processes": suggestions.processes,
            "rationale": suggestions.rationale
        }

3. Frontend интеграция с AI Advisor
typescript// frontend/components/BIA/BIAWorkspace.tsx

export function BIAWorkspace({ biaId }: { biaId: string }) {
  const [context, setContext] = useState<WorkflowContext>();
  const [aiAdvice, setAiAdvice] = useState<AIAdvice>();
  
  // Загрузить контекст при открытии
  useEffect(() => {
    loadWorkflowContext();
    requestProactiveAdvice();
  }, [biaId]);
  
  async function loadWorkflowContext() {
    const ctx = await fetch(`/api/bia/${biaId}/context`).then(r => r.json());
    setContext(ctx);
  }
  
  async function requestProactiveAdvice() {
    // AI Advisor даёт проактивные советы
    const advice = await fetch(`/api/ai/bia/${biaId}/advice`).then(r => r.json());
    setAiAdvice(advice);
  }
  
  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen">
      {/* Основная рабочая область */}
      <div className="flex-1">
        <WorkflowProgress 
          stage={context?.current_stage}
          percentage={context?.progress_percentage}
        />
        
        {/* Контент зависит от стадии */}
        {context?.current_stage === 'identify_processes' && (
          <ProcessIdentificationView 
            biaId={biaId}
            processes={context.current_stage_data.processes}
            onAddProcess={handleAddProcess}
          />
        )}
        
        {context?.current_stage === 'assess_impact' && (
          <ImpactAssessmentView
            biaId={biaId}
            processes={context.current_stage_data.processes}
          />
        )}
      </div>
      
      {/* AI Advisor Sidebar */}
      <div className="w-96 border-l">
        <AIAdvisorPanel
          advice={aiAdvice}
          context={context}
          onAction={handleAIAction}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function AIAdvisorPanel({ advice, context, onAction }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col h-full">
      {/* Текущая стадия */}
      <div className="p-4 border-b bg-blue-50">
        <h3 className="font-semibold">Current Stage</h3>
        <p className="text-sm">{context?.current_stage_label}</p>
        <div className="mt-2">
          <Progress value={context?.progress_percentage} />
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* AI Advice */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4">
        <div className="mb-4">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
            <Avatar>🤖</Avatar>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="text-sm whitespace-pre-line">
                {advice?.message}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        
        {/* Gaps (что не хватает) */}
        {context?.gaps?.length > 0 && (
          <Alert variant="warning" className="mb-4">
            <AlertTitle>What's Missing</AlertTitle>
            <AlertDescription>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-4">
                {context.gaps.map(gap => (
                  <li key={gap.field}>{gap.message}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}
        
        {/* Issues */}
        {context?.issues?.length > 0 && (
          <Alert variant="destructive" className="mb-4">
            <AlertTitle>Issues Detected</AlertTitle>
            <AlertDescription>
              <ul className="list-disc pl-4">
                {context.issues.map(issue => (
                  <li key={issue.id}>{issue.message}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        )}
      </div>
      
      {/* Quick Actions */}
      <div className="p-4 border-t">
        <h4 className="font-semibold mb-2">Suggested Actions</h4>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          {context?.available_actions?.map(action => (
            <Button
              key={action.id}
              variant={action.type === 'primary' ? 'default' : 'outline'}
              className="w-full"
              onClick={() => onAction(action.id)}
            >
              {action.label}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
      
      {/* Chat with AI */}
      <div className="p-4 border-t">
        <input
          placeholder="Ask AI Advisor..."
          onKeyPress={(e) => {
            if (e.key === 'Enter') {
              handleAskAI(e.currentTarget.value);
            }
          }}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

4. EventBus следит за прогрессом
python# services/bia/events.py

@eventbus.subscribe("bia.process.added")
async def on_process_added(event: Event):
    """Когда добавлен процесс, проверить можно ли двигаться дальше"""
    
    workflow = BIAWorkflow(event.data["bia_id"])
    context = await workflow.get_current_context()
    
    # Если достигнут минимум процессов, предложить следующий шаг
    if len(context["current_stage_data"]["processes"]) >= 3:
        await eventbus.publish("bia.ready_for_next_stage", {
            "bia_id": event.data["bia_id"],
            "current_stage": context["current_stage"],
            "next_stage": "analyze_dependencies"
        })
        
        # AI Advisor автоматически уведомит пользователя
        await ai_advisor.notify_milestone_reached(
            bia_id=event.data["bia_id"],
            milestone="minimum_processes_reached"
        )

Итоговая картина: AI + Workflow
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  USER работает в BIA модуле                    │
│  - Добавляет процессы                          │
│  - Заполняет поля                              │
│  - Смотрит на AI Advisor                       │
└───────────────┬────────────────────────────────┘
                │
                ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  WORKFLOW STATE MACHINE                        │
│  - Знает текущую стадию                        │
│  - Знает что сделано / не сделано              │
│  - Знает какие действия доступны               │
│  - Публикует события в EventBus                │
└───────────────┬────────────────────────────────┘
                │
                │ Предоставляет контекст
                ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  AI ADVISOR                                    │
│  - Получает полный контекст workflow           │
│  - Анализирует gaps/issues                     │
│  - Даёт конкретные советы                      │
│  - Предлагает действия                         │
│  - Отвечает на вопросы В КОНТЕКСТЕ             │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
Ключевое преимущество: AI не галлюцинирует что делать дальше, потому что workflow state machine даёт ему чёткий контекст и доступные действия.
Это то что ты имел в виду?RetryMда! спасибо! а если собрать уже библиотеку это поможет и собирать кейсы от процессаEditДА! Точно! Это мощная идея:
Workflow State + Case Library = Self-Learning Platform
Каждый completed workflow → записывается как case
    ↓
Case Library накапливает знания
    ↓
AI Advisor учится на реальных примерах
    ↓
Новые пользователи получают advice на основе успешных cases

Архитектура Case Library
1. Структура Case
python# models/case.py

class WorkflowCase(BaseModel):
    """Случай успешного прохождения workflow"""
    
    # Идентификация
    case_id: str
    module: str  # "bia", "risk", "planning"
    workflow_name: str  # "bia_process", "risk_assessment"
    
    # Контекст организации (anonymized)
    organization_context: dict = {
        "industry": "healthcare",
        "size": "medium",
        "org_type": "hospital",
        "maturity_level": "basic",
        # NO identifiable info (names, emails, etc)
    }
    
    # Полный путь через workflow
    journey: List[WorkflowStep] = [
        {
            "stage": "identify_processes",
            "started_at": "2025-01-15T10:00:00Z",
            "completed_at": "2025-01-18T16:30:00Z",
            "duration_hours": 78,
            "actions_taken": [
                {
                    "action": "add_process",
                    "data": {"name": "Emergency Department", "tier": 1},
                    "timestamp": "2025-01-15T10:30:00Z"
                },
                {
                    "action": "ai_suggest_processes",
                    "ai_suggestions_accepted": 8,
                    "timestamp": "2025-01-16T09:00:00Z"
                }
            ],
            "challenges": [
                {
                    "type": "insufficient_data",
                    "description": "User initially identified only 2 processes",
                    "resolution": "AI suggested 10 typical healthcare processes",
                    "time_to_resolve_hours": 24
                }
            ]
        },
        {
            "stage": "analyze_dependencies",
            "started_at": "2025-01-18T16:30:00Z",
            "completed_at": "2025-01-22T14:00:00Z",
            "duration_hours": 93,
            # ...
        }
    ]
    
    # Метрики успеха
    metrics: dict = {
        "total_duration_days": 14,
        "processes_identified": 12,
        "critical_processes": 4,
        "ai_recommendations_used": 15,
        "user_satisfaction": 4.5,  # 1-5 scale
        "completed_successfully": True,
        "certification_ready": True
    }
    
    # Patterns (что сработало хорошо)
    success_patterns: List[str] = [
        "Used AI to suggest processes early - saved 2 days",
        "Involved process owners in dependency analysis",
        "Conducted mini-workshop for impact assessment",
        "Used industry templates for RTO determination"
    ]
    
    # Lessons learned
    lessons_learned: List[str] = [
        "Start with critical processes first",
        "Don't skip dependency mapping - critical for RTO",
        "AI recommendations need validation by subject matter experts"
    ]
    
    # Для ML: features
    features: dict = {
        "industry": "healthcare",
        "size": "medium",
        "experience_level": "beginner",
        "ai_assistance_level": "high",
        "team_size": 2,
        "has_consultant": False
    }
    
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime

class WorkflowStep(BaseModel):
    stage: str
    started_at: datetime
    completed_at: datetime
    duration_hours: float
    actions_taken: List[dict]
    challenges: List[dict]
    ai_interactions: List[dict]

2. Case Collection (автоматический сбор)
python# services/case_collector.py

class CaseCollector:
    """Автоматически собирает cases из workflow"""
    
    @eventbus.subscribe("*.workflow.step.completed")
    async def on_workflow_step_completed(self, event: Event):
        """
        Записывает каждый шаг workflow
        """
        await db.save_workflow_step(
            org_id=event.data["org_id"],
            module=event.data["module"],
            step=WorkflowStep(
                stage=event.data["stage"],
                started_at=event.data["started_at"],
                completed_at=event.data["completed_at"],
                duration_hours=calculate_duration(
                    event.data["started_at"],
                    event.data["completed_at"]
                ),
                actions_taken=event.data["actions"],
                challenges=event.data.get("challenges", [])
            )
        )
    
    @eventbus.subscribe("*.workflow.completed")
    async def on_workflow_completed(self, event: Event):
        """
        Когда workflow завершён, создаём case для library
        """
        org_id = event.data["org_id"]
        module = event.data["module"]
        
        # 1. Собрать все шаги
        steps = await db.get_workflow_steps(org_id, module)
        
        # 2. Собрать метрики
        metrics = await self._calculate_metrics(org_id, module, steps)
        
        # 3. Извлечь patterns (что работало хорошо)
        patterns = await self._extract_success_patterns(steps)
        
        # 4. Anonymize organization context
        org_context = await self._get_anonymized_context(org_id)
        
        # 5. Создать case
        case = WorkflowCase(
            case_id=generate_id(),
            module=module,
            workflow_name=event.data["workflow_name"],
            organization_context=org_context,
            journey=steps,
            metrics=metrics,
            success_patterns=patterns,
            lessons_learned=event.data.get("lessons_learned", []),
            features=self._extract_features(org_context, steps, metrics)
        )
        
        # 6. Сохранить в Case Library
        await case_library.save(case)
        
        # 7. Trigger ML model retraining (async)
        asyncio.create_task(
            ml_service.retrain_recommendation_model()
        )
    
    async def _extract_success_patterns(
        self,
        steps: List[WorkflowStep]
    ) -> List[str]:
        """
        AI анализирует шаги и находит что сработало хорошо
        """
        prompt = f"""
        Analyze this workflow journey and identify success patterns:
        
        {json.dumps([step.dict() for step in steps], indent=2)}
        
        Identify:
        1. Actions that significantly accelerated progress
        2. AI recommendations that were valuable
        3. Best practices demonstrated
        4. Effective problem-solving approaches
        
        Format as bullet points.
        """
        
        analysis = await llm_generate(prompt=prompt)
        return analysis.patterns

3. Case Library Service
python# services/case_library.py

class CaseLibrary:
    """Хранилище и поиск cases"""
    
    async def save(self, case: WorkflowCase):
        """Сохранить case в БД + Vector DB"""
        
        # 1. PostgreSQL (structured data)
        await db.cases.create(case)
        
        # 2. Vector DB (для semantic search)
        embedding = await embed_case(case)
        await vector_db.upsert(
            id=case.case_id,
            vector=embedding,
            metadata={
                "module": case.module,
                "industry": case.organization_context["industry"],
                "size": case.organization_context["size"],
                "duration_days": case.metrics["total_duration_days"],
                "success_score": case.metrics.get("user_satisfaction", 0)
            }
        )
    
    async def find_similar_cases(
        self,
        org_context: dict,
        module: str,
        current_stage: str,
        limit: int = 5
    ) -> List[WorkflowCase]:
        """
        Найти похожие cases для обучения
        """
        
        # 1. Build search query
        query = f"""
        Organization: {org_context['industry']}, {org_context['size']}
        Module: {module}
        Current stage: {current_stage}
        Looking for: successful workflow examples
        """
        
        # 2. Semantic search
        results = await vector_db.query(
            query=query,
            filter={
                "module": module,
                "industry": org_context["industry"]
            },
            top_k=limit
        )
        
        # 3. Load full cases
        cases = []
        for result in results:
            case = await db.cases.get(result.id)
            cases.append(case)
        
        return cases
    
    async def get_benchmarks(
        self,
        industry: str,
        module: str
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Статистика по всем cases (benchmarking)
        """
        cases = await db.cases.filter(
            module=module,
            industry=industry
        )
        
        return {
            "total_cases": len(cases),
            "avg_duration_days": np.mean([c.metrics["total_duration_days"] for c in cases]),
            "median_duration_days": np.median([c.metrics["total_duration_days"] for c in cases]),
            "success_rate": len([c for c in cases if c.metrics["completed_successfully"]]) / len(cases),
            "common_challenges": self._aggregate_challenges(cases),
            "best_practices": self._aggregate_patterns(cases),
            "ai_usage_correlation": self._correlate_ai_usage_with_success(cases)
        }

4. AI Advisor использует Case Library
python# ai/advisor.py (updated)

class BIAAdvisor:
    
    async def get_contextual_advice(
        self,
        bia_id: str,
        user_message: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Advice на основе:
        1. Текущего состояния workflow
        2. Похожих успешных cases
        """
        
        # 1. Текущий контекст
        workflow = BIAWorkflow(bia_id)
        context = await workflow.get_current_context()
        
        # 2. Найти похожие successful cases
        org = await db.get_organization(context["org_id"])
        similar_cases = await case_library.find_similar_cases(
            org_context={
                "industry": org.industry,
                "size": org.size,
                "maturity_level": org.bcm_maturity
            },
            module="bia",
            current_stage=context["current_stage"],
            limit=3
        )
        
        # 3. Benchmarks
        benchmarks = await case_library.get_benchmarks(
            industry=org.industry,
            module="bia"
        )
        
        # 4. Build prompt с real examples
        prompt = f"""
You are a BCM expert helping with Business Impact Analysis.

CURRENT SITUATION:
{json.dumps(context, indent=2)}

SIMILAR SUCCESSFUL CASES (learn from these):
{self._format_cases_for_prompt(similar_cases)}

INDUSTRY BENCHMARKS:
- Average BIA duration: {benchmarks["avg_duration_days"]} days
- Your progress: {context["progress_percentage"]}%
- Common challenges in your industry: {benchmarks["common_challenges"]}
- Best practices: {benchmarks["best_practices"]}

USER MESSAGE: {user_message or "Provide proactive guidance"}

YOUR TASK:
1. Compare user's progress to similar successful cases
2. Identify if they're on track or struggling
3. Suggest specific actions based on what worked for similar organizations
4. Warn about common pitfalls in their industry
5. Be encouraging but realistic

Use examples from similar cases to make advice concrete.
"""
        
        response = await llm_generate(prompt=prompt)
        
        return {
            "message": response.text,
            "similar_cases": [
                {
                    "case_id": c.case_id,
                    "organization": f"{c.organization_context['industry']} ({c.organization_context['size']})",
                    "duration_days": c.metrics["total_duration_days"],
                    "success_patterns": c.success_patterns[:3],
                    "view_url": f"/cases/{c.case_id}"
                }
                for c in similar_cases
            ],
            "benchmarks": benchmarks
        }
    
    def _format_cases_for_prompt(
        self,
        cases: List[WorkflowCase]
    ) -> str:
        """Format cases для LLM prompt"""
        
        formatted = []
        for i, case in enumerate(cases, 1):
            formatted.append(f"""
Case {i}: {case.organization_context['industry']} organization
- Completed in: {case.metrics['total_duration_days']} days
- Processes identified: {case.metrics['processes_identified']}
- What worked well:
  {chr(10).join(f"  • {p}" for p in case.success_patterns[:3])}
- Challenges faced:
  {chr(10).join(f"  • {c['description']}: {c['resolution']}" for c in case.journey[0].get('challenges', [])[:2])}
""")
        
        return "\n".join(formatted)

typescript// components/BIA/AIAdvisorPanel.tsx

function AIAdvisorPanel({ advice, context }) {
  return (
    <div>
      {/* AI Advice */}
      <div className="p-4">
        <p>{advice.message}</p>
      </div>
      
      {/* Similar Cases */}
      {advice.similar_cases?.length > 0 && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-blue-50">
          <h4 className="font-semibold mb-2">
            📚 Learn from Similar Organizations
          </h4>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {advice.similar_cases.map(case => (
              <Card key={case.case_id}>
                <CardContent className="p-3">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-sm font-medium">
                        {case.organization}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">
                        Completed in {case.duration_days} days
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <Badge>Success</Badge>
                  </div>
                  
                  <div className="mt-2">
                    <p className="text-xs font-medium mb-1">What worked:</p>
                    <ul className="text-xs space-y-1">
                      {case.success_patterns.map((pattern, i) => (
                        <li key={i} className="flex items-start gap-1">
                          <span>✓</span>
                          <span>{pattern}</span>
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                  
                  <Button
                    variant="link"
                    size="sm"
                    onClick={() => window.open(case.view_url)}
                  >
                    View Full Case →
                  </Button>
                </CardContent>
              </Card>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      
      {/* Benchmarks */}
      {advice.benchmarks && (
        <div className="p-4 border-t">
          <h4 className="font-semibold mb-2">📊 Industry Benchmarks</h4>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-sm">
            <div>
              <p className="text-gray-600">Avg Duration</p>
              <p className="font-semibold">
                {advice.benchmarks.avg_duration_days} days
              </p>
            </div>
            <div>
              <p className="text-gray-600">Success Rate</p>
              <p className="font-semibold">
                {(advice.benchmarks.success_rate * 100).toFixed(0)}%
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <div className="mt-2">
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">
              You're {context.progress_percentage}% complete
              {context.progress_percentage > 50 ? " - ahead of schedule! 🎉" : ""}
            </p>
            <Progress value={context.progress_percentage} className="mt-1" />
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

6. ML Model: предсказание проблем
python# ml/workflow_predictor.py

class WorkflowPredictor:
    """
    ML модель предсказывает:
    - Будет ли пользователь успешен?
    - Сколько времени займёт?
    - Какие проблемы вероятны?
    """
    
    async def train(self):
        """Обучить модель на case library"""
        
        # Load all cases
        cases = await db.cases.all()
        
        # Prepare features
        X = []
        y_success = []
        y_duration = []
        
        for case in cases:
            features = [
                self._encode_industry(case.features["industry"]),
                self._encode_size(case.features["size"]),
                case.features["experience_level"],
                case.features["ai_assistance_level"],
                case.features["team_size"],
                case.features["has_consultant"]
            ]
            X.append(features)
            y_success.append(case.metrics["completed_successfully"])
            y_duration.append(case.metrics["total_duration_days"])
        
        # Train models
        self.success_model = RandomForestClassifier()
        self.success_model.fit(X, y_success)
        
        self.duration_model = RandomForestRegressor()
        self.duration_model.fit(X, y_duration)
        
        # Save models
        joblib.dump(self.success_model, "models/workflow_success.pkl")
        joblib.dump(self.duration_model, "models/workflow_duration.pkl")
    
    async def predict(
        self,
        org_context: dict,
        current_progress: dict
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Предсказать вероятность успеха и duration
        """
        features = self._prepare_features(org_context, current_progress)
        
        success_prob = self.success_model.predict_proba(features)[0][1]
        estimated_duration = self.duration_model.predict(features)[0]
        
        # Identify risk factors
        risk_factors = await self._identify_risks(
            org_context,
            current_progress,
            success_prob
        )
        
        return {
            "success_probability": success_prob,
            "estimated_duration_days": estimated_duration,
            "risk_level": "high" if success_prob < 0.7 else "medium" if success_prob < 0.85 else "low",
            "risk_factors": risk_factors,
            "recommendations": self._generate_recommendations(risk_factors)
        }

Итоговая картина: Self-Learning Platform
Пользователи работают → Workflow States записываются
                              ↓
                         Case Library
                              ↓
                    ┌─────────┴─────────┐
                    ▼                   ▼
            AI Advisor           ML Predictor
            учится на            предсказывает
            примерах             проблемы
                    ↓                   ↓
            Новые пользователи получают:
            - Advice на основе real cases
            - Предупреждения о рисках
            - Benchmarks vs. похожие org
            - Best practices from successful cases
Преимущества:

AI не галлюцинирует - советы основаны на real cases
Platform улучшается - чем больше пользователей, тем умнее AI
Benchmarking - пользователи видят как они vs. industry
Predictive - ML предсказывает проблемы до их появления
Privacy-preserving - cases anonymized

KEY DATA FLOWS
Flow 1: User conducts BIA with AI assistance
1. USER opens BIA module in Web App
   ↓
2. Frontend loads workflow state from API Gateway
   GET /api/bia/{org_id}/workflow-state
   ↓
3. API Gateway → BIA Service → Returns current state
   {
     current_stage: "identify_processes",
     progress: 25%,
     gaps: [...],
     available_actions: [...]
   }
   ↓
4. Frontend requests AI advice
   POST /api/ai/bia/{bia_id}/advice
   ↓
5. API Gateway → AI Service → Calls:
   - Workflow state (from BIA Service)
   - Similar cases (from Case Library)
   - Benchmarks (from Case Library)
   ↓
6. AI Service → MCP BCM Expert (if needed)
   tool: recommend_rto
   ↓
7. MCP Server:
   - Queries Knowledge Graph (ISO requirements)
   - Searches Case Library (similar successful examples)
   - Queries Vector DB (RAG for context)
   - Calls LLM with full context
   ↓
8. AI advice returned to Frontend with:
   - Message with specific suggestions
   - Similar cases to learn from
   - Benchmarks (how they compare)
   - Quick actions
   ↓
9. User clicks action → Frontend → API Gateway → BIA Service
   ↓
10. BIA Service:
    - Updates workflow state
    - Publishes event "bia.process.added"
    ↓
11. EventBus broadcasts to:
    - Case Collector (records action)
    - ML Predictor (updates predictions)
    - Compliance Service (checks requirements)
    - Digital Twin (updates model)
    ↓
12. Frontend receives WebSocket update
    - UI updates in real-time
    - AI Advisor refreshes with new context
Flow 2: External user (Claude Desktop) via MCP
1. USER in Claude Desktop:
   "Recommend RTO for Emergency Department in my hospital"
   ↓
2. Claude detects need for bcm-ai-expert MCP tool
   ↓
3. MCP Client → BCM Expert MCP Server
   recommend_rto(org_id="hospital_001", process="ED")
   ↓
4. MCP Server:
   a) GET Platform API → Organization context
   b) GET Platform API → Existing BIA data
   c) Query Knowledge Graph → ISO requirements
   d) Search Case Library → Similar healthcare orgs
   e) Query Vector DB → RAG retrieval
   f) LLM analysis with all context
   g) POST Platform API → Save RTO recommendation
   ↓
5. MCP Server returns to Claude:
   {
     recommended_rto: "0 hours",
     rationale: "...",
     similar_cases: [...],
     gaps_identified: [...]
   }
   ↓
6. Claude shows user formatted response with:
   - Recommendation
   - Explanation
   - Similar examples
   - Link to platform to view full BIA
   ↓
7. Platform receives saved data via API
   - Triggers EventBus events
   - Updates workflow state
   - Case Collector records action
Flow 3: Workflow completion → Case creation
1. USER completes final BIA step
   ↓
2. BIA Service detects all requirements met
   ↓
3. BIA Service transitions workflow to "completed"
   await workflow.transition_to(BIAStage.COMPLETED)
   ↓
4. Publishes event "bia.workflow.completed"
   ↓
5. Case Collector subscribes to event:
   a) Collects all workflow steps from DB
   b) Calculates metrics
   c) AI extracts success patterns
   d) Anonymizes organization context
   e) Creates WorkflowCase object
   ↓
6. Case Library saves case:
   a) PostgreSQL (structured data)
   b) Vector DB (for semantic search)
   ↓
7. ML Predictor triggered:
   - Retrains models with new case
   - Updates success probability predictions
   ↓
8. Future users benefit:
   - AI Advisor references this case
   - Benchmarks updated
   - Success patterns available

INTEGRATION POINTS
1. Workflow State ↔ AI Advisor
python# services/bia/workflow.py
class BIAWorkflow:
    async def get_ai_context(self) -> dict:
        """Context specifically for AI"""
        return {
            "workflow_state": await self.get_current_context(),
            "similar_cases": await case_library.find_similar(self.org_context),
            "benchmarks": await case_library.get_benchmarks(self.industry),
            "knowledge_base": await knowledge_graph.get_relevant(self.current_stage)
        }

# ai/advisor.py  
class AIAdvisor:
    async def advise(self, bia_id: str):
        workflow = BIAWorkflow(bia_id)
        context = await workflow.get_ai_context()
        
        # Full context to LLM
        advice = await llm_generate(
            prompt=self.build_prompt(context)
        )
        return advice
2. EventBus ↔ Case Collector
python# All workflow events flow through EventBus
@eventbus.subscribe("*.workflow.step.completed")
async def on_step_completed(event):
    await case_collector.record_step(event)

@eventbus.subscribe("*.workflow.completed")
async def on_workflow_completed(event):
    case = await case_collector.create_case(event)
    await case_library.save(case)
    await ml_predictor.retrain()
3. MCP Server ↔ Platform API
python# MCP Server always syncs with platform
@mcp_server.tool("recommend_rto")
async def recommend_rto(org_id: str, process: str):
    # 1. Get platform data
    org = await platform_api.get(f"/organizations/{org_id}")
    bia = await platform_api.get(f"/bia/{org_id}")
    
    # 2. Get meta-intelligence
    knowledge = await knowledge_graph.query(...)
    cases = await case_library.search(...)
    
    # 3. AI analysis
    recommendation = await llm_analyze(org, bia, knowledge, cases)
    
    # 4. Save back to platform
    await platform_api.post(f"/bia/{org_id}/rto", recommendation)
    
    return recommendation
4. Knowledge Graph ↔ Case Library
python# Case Library learns from patterns
class CaseLibrary:
    async def extract_patterns(self, case: WorkflowCase):
        # AI identifies what worked
        patterns = await llm_extract_patterns(case.journey)
        
        # Store in Knowledge Graph as new best practices
        for pattern in patterns:
            await knowledge_graph.add_best_practice(
                industry=case.industry,
                pattern=pattern,
                evidence=case.case_id
            )

DEPLOYMENT
Docker Compose (Development/Staging)
yamlversion: '3.8'

services:
  # Infrastructure
  postgres:
    image: postgres:15-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: bcm_platform
      POSTGRES_USER: bcm
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD}
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
  
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
  
  neo4j:
    image: neo4j:5-community
    environment:
      NEO4J_AUTH: neo4j/${NEO4J_PASSWORD}
    ports:
      - "7474:7474"
      - "7687:7687"
    volumes:
      - neo4j_data:/data
  
  minio:
    image: minio/minio
    command: server /data --console-address ":9001"
    environment:
      MINIO_ROOT_USER: ${MINIO_USER}
      MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD: ${MINIO_PASSWORD}
    ports:
      - "9000:9000"
      - "9001:9001"
  
  # Core Platform
  api-gateway:
    build: ./api_gateway
    ports:
      - "8000:8000"
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379
    depends_on:
      - postgres
      - redis
  
  orchestrator:
    build: ./orchestrator
    ports:
      - "8002:8002"
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379
  
  eventbus:
    build: ./eventbus
    ports:
      - "8001:8001"
    environment:
      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379
  
  # BCM Services
  governance:
    build: ./services/governance
    environment:
      SERVICE_PORT: 8010
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      EVENTBUS_URL: http://eventbus:8001
  
  bia:
    build: ./services/bia
    environment:
      SERVICE_PORT: 8011
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      EVENTBUS_URL: http://eventbus:8001
  
  # ... other services
  
  # Case Library & ML
  case-collector:
    build: ./services/case_collector
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
      EVENTBUS_URL: http://eventbus:8001
  
  ml-predictor:
    build: ./services/ml_predictor
    environment:
      DATABASE_URL: postgresql://bcm:${DB_PASSWORD}@postgres:5432/bcm_platform
  
  # MCP Servers
  mcp-bcm-expert:
    build: ./mcp_servers/bcm_expert
    environment:
      PLATFORM_API_URL: http://api-gateway:8000
      NEO4J_URL: bolt://neo4j:7687
      OPENAI_API_KEY: ${OPENAI_API_KEY}
      ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${ANTHROPIC_API_KEY}
    depends_on:
      - api-gateway
      - neo4j
  
  # Frontend
  web-app:
    build: ./frontend
    ports:
      - "3000:3000"
    environment:
      NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL: http://api-gateway:8000
      NEXT_PUBLIC_WS_URL: ws://eventbus:8001
  
  # Monitoring
  prometheus:
    image: prom/prometheus
    ports:
      - "9090:9090"
    volumes:
      - ./monitoring/prometheus.yml:/etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml
  
  grafana:
    image: grafana/grafana
    ports:
      - "3001:3000"
    environment:
      GF_SECURITY_ADMIN_PASSWORD: ${GRAFANA_PASSWORD}

volumes:
  postgres_data:
  redis_data:
  neo4j_data:
  minio_data:

КРИТИЧЕСКИЕ СВЯЗИ
1. Workflow State Machine → AI Advisor
Workflow предоставляет AI полный контекст:
- Где пользователь сейчас
- Что сделано / не сделано
- Какие gaps
- Доступные действия

AI не может галлюцинировать неверные советы
2. Case Library → AI Advice
AI использует real examples вместо generic advice:
"Organization like yours (healthcare, 200 beds) 
completed BIA in 14 days using these approaches:
1. Started with critical processes (ED, Surgery)
2. Used AI to suggest dependencies
3. ..."

Concrete, actionable, proven
3. EventBus → Case Collector
Каждое действие пользователя автоматически записывается:
- Добавил процесс → recorded
- Запросил AI recommendation → recorded
- Столкнулся с проблемой → recorded
- Решил проблему → recorded

Platform учится без explicit user action
4. MCP Servers → Platform API
External AI (Claude Desktop) и Internal AI (embedded)
используют ОДНИ И ТЕ ЖЕ инструменты через MCP:
- Consistency
- Single source of truth
- Platform владеет данными
5. Knowledge Graph → Case Library
Bidirectional learning:
- Cases → extract patterns → add to Knowledge Graph
- Knowledge Graph → inform Case search
- Continuous improvement

Это ПОЛНАЯ ФИНАЛЬНАЯ АРХИТЕКТУРА. Все части связаны, всё работает вместе:
✅ Workflow State Machine знает где пользователь
✅ AI Advisor получает полный контекст (state + cases + knowledge)
✅ Case Library накапливает опыт и учит AI
✅ MCP серверы дают AI capabilities как внутри так и снаружи
✅ EventBus связывает всё в единую систему
✅ Platform владеет данными и бизнес-логикой

🎯 СТРАТЕГИЯ ИНТЕГРАЦИИ
Phase 1: Foundation (Неделя 1-2)
1.1 Поднять инфраструктуру
Создать docker-compose.dev.yml для локальной разработки:
yamlversion: '3.8'

services:
  # Core Infrastructure
  postgres:
    image: postgres:15-alpine
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: bcm_platform
      POSTGRES_USER: bcm
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: bcm_dev_pass
    ports:
      - "5432:5432"
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
  
  redis:
    image: redis:7-alpine
    ports:
      - "6379:6379"
  
  neo4j:
    image: neo4j:5-community
    environment:
      NEO4J_AUTH: neo4j/neo4j_dev_pass
      NEO4J_PLUGINS: '["apoc", "graph-data-science"]'
    ports:
      - "7474:7474"
      - "7687:7687"
    volumes:
      - neo4j_data:/data
  
  minio:
    image: minio/minio
    command: server /data --console-address ":9001"
    environment:
      MINIO_ROOT_USER: bcm_dev
      MINIO_ROOT_PASSWORD: bcm_dev_pass
    ports:
      - "9000:9000"
      - "9001:9001"
    volumes:
      - minio_data:/data

volumes:
  postgres_data:
  neo4j_data:
  minio_data:

PINECONE_API_KEY=your_key

# JWT
JWT_SECRET=dev_secret_key_change_in_prod
1.2 Database Migrations
Создать Alembic migrations для всех сервисов:
bash# В каждом сервисе
cd services/governance
alembic init alembic
alembic revision --autogenerate -m "initial"
alembic upgrade head
Unified schema с multi-tenancy:
python# shared/database/base.py

from sqlalchemy import Column, String, DateTime, Boolean, ForeignKey
from sqlalchemy.ext.declarative import declarative_base
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import UUID
import uuid
from datetime import datetime

Base = declarative_base()

class TenantMixin:
    """Multi-tenant support"""
    org_id = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True), nullable=False, index=True)

class TimestampMixin:
    """Timestamp fields"""
    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, nullable=False)
    updated_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow, onupdate=datetime.utcnow)

class AuditMixin:
    """Audit trail"""
    created_by = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True))
    updated_by = Column(UUID(as_uuid=True))
    is_deleted = Column(Boolean, default=False)
1.3 Запустить Core Services
Порядок запуска:
bash# 1. Infrastructure
docker-compose -f docker-compose.dev.yml up -d

# 2. Core Platform Services
cd services/eventbus && python main.py &
cd services/orchestrator && python main.py &
cd services/api_gateway && python main.py &

# 3. BCM Services (по одному, тестируем)
cd services/governance && python main.py &
cd services/bia && python main.py &

Phase 2: Case Library (Неделя 3)
2.1 Создать Case Library Service
Новый сервис:
services/
└── case_library/
    ├── main.py
    ├── models/
    │   └── case.py          # WorkflowCase model
    ├── services/
    │   ├── collector.py     # Case collection
    │   ├── library.py       # Storage & search
