EVENTBUS - ПРАВИЛЬНАЯ АРХИТЕКТУРА
Философия дизайна:

Простой интерфейс (не меняется никогда)
Pluggable backend (in-memory → Redis → RabbitMQ)
Type-safe события
Ноль внешних зависимостей для core


CORE: Event System Interface
python# eventbus/core/events.py

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Dict, Optional
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
import uuid

class EventPriority(Enum):
    LOW = 1
    NORMAL = 2
    HIGH = 3
    CRITICAL = 4

@dataclass
class Event:
    """
    Base event - используется везде в платформе
    
    Не зависит от транспорта (можно слать куда угодно)
    """
    # Identity
    id: str
    type: str  # 'workflow.stage_changed', 'bia.process_added'
    
    # Payload
    data: Dict[str, Any]
    
    # Context
    source: str  # 'workflow-engine', 'ai-advisor'
    tenant_id: str
    correlation_id: Optional[str] = None  # For tracing
    
    # Metadata
    timestamp: datetime
    priority: EventPriority = EventPriority.NORMAL
    
    # Retry
    retry_count: int = 0
    max_retries: int = 3
    
    @classmethod
    def create(
        cls,
        event_type: str,
        data: Dict[str, Any],
        source: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        priority: EventPriority = EventPriority.NORMAL
    ) -> 'Event':
        """Factory для создания событий"""
        return cls(
            id=str(uuid.uuid4()),
            type=event_type,
            data=data,
            source=source,
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
            priority=priority
        )

CORE: EventBus Interface (контракт)
python# eventbus/core/interface.py

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from typing import Callable, Awaitable, List, Optional
from .events import Event

EventHandler = Callable[[Event], Awaitable[None]]

class IEventBus(ABC):
    """
    Интерфейс EventBus - не зависит от реализации
    
    Любой транспорт должен реализовать этот интерфейс
    """
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def publish(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """
        Publish event
        
        Гарантии:
        - At-least-once delivery (может быть дубли)
        - Async (не блокирует)
        """
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def subscribe(
        self,
        event_type: str,
        handler: EventHandler,
        consumer_group: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> str:
        """
        Subscribe to event type
        
        Args:
            event_type: 'workflow.*' supports wildcards
            handler: async function to call
            consumer_group: для load balancing между инстансами
            
        Returns:
            subscription_id для unsubscribe
        """
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def unsubscribe(self, subscription_id: str) -> None:
        """Cancel subscription"""
        pass
    
    @abstractmethod
    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Cleanup resources"""
        pass
    
    # Optional (для мониторинга)
    async def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Get bus statistics"""
        return {
            'published': 0,
            'consumed': 0,
            'errors': 0
        }

IMPLEMENTATION 1: In-Memory (для MVP/тестов)
python# eventbus/backends/memory.py

import asyncio
from typing import Dict, List, Set
import re
from ..core.interface import IEventBus, EventHandler
from ..core.events import Event

class InMemoryEventBus(IEventBus):
    """
    In-memory event bus для MVP и тестов
    
    Плюсы:
    - Ноль зависимостей
    - Instant startup
    - Идеально для unit tests
    
    Минусы:
    - Не переживает restart
    - Не работает между процессами
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self._handlers: Dict[str, List[EventHandler]] = {}
        self._subscriptions: Dict[str, tuple] = {}  # sub_id -> (pattern, handler)
        self._stats = {
            'published': 0,
            'consumed': 0,
            'errors': 0
        }
        
    async def publish(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Publish to all matching handlers"""
        self._stats['published'] += 1
        
        # Find matching handlers
        handlers = self._find_handlers(event.type)
        
        # Call handlers (async, don't wait)
        for handler in handlers:
            asyncio.create_task(self._safe_handle(event, handler))
            
    async def _safe_handle(self, event: Event, handler: EventHandler):
        """Handle with error catching"""
        try:
            await handler(event)
            self._stats['consumed'] += 1
        except Exception as e:
            self._stats['errors'] += 1
            # Log error (inject logger later)
            print(f"Handler error: {e}")
            
            # Retry logic
            if event.retry_count < event.max_retries:
                event.retry_count += 1
                await asyncio.sleep(2 ** event.retry_count)  # Exponential backoff
                await self.publish(event)
    
    async def subscribe(
        self,
        event_type: str,
        handler: EventHandler,
        consumer_group: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> str:
        """Subscribe to pattern"""
        import uuid
        
        sub_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
        
        # Store subscription
        if event_type not in self._handlers:
            self._handlers[event_type] = []
        self._handlers[event_type].append(handler)
        
        self._subscriptions[sub_id] = (event_type, handler)
        
        return sub_id
    
    async def unsubscribe(self, subscription_id: str) -> None:
        """Remove subscription"""
        if subscription_id in self._subscriptions:
            event_type, handler = self._subscriptions[subscription_id]
            if event_type in self._handlers:
                self._handlers[event_type].remove(handler)
            del self._subscriptions[subscription_id]
    
    def _find_handlers(self, event_type: str) -> List[EventHandler]:
        """Find handlers matching event type (with wildcard support)"""
        handlers = []
        
        for pattern, handler_list in self._handlers.items():
            if self._match_pattern(pattern, event_type):
                handlers.extend(handler_list)
                
        return handlers
    
    def _match_pattern(self, pattern: str, event_type: str) -> bool:
        """Match event type against pattern (supports *)"""
        # Convert glob to regex
        regex = pattern.replace('.', r'\.').replace('*', '.*')
        return re.match(f'^{regex}$', event_type) is not None
    
    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Cleanup"""
        self._handlers.clear()
        self._subscriptions.clear()
        
    async def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        return self._stats.copy()

IMPLEMENTATION 2: Redis Streams (production-ready)
python# eventbus/backends/redis_streams.py

import json
import asyncio
from typing import Optional
import redis.asyncio as redis
from ..core.interface import IEventBus, EventHandler
from ..core.events import Event, EventPriority

class RedisStreamEventBus(IEventBus):
    """
    Redis Streams event bus
    
    Плюсы:
    - Персистентность
    - Consumer groups (load balancing)
    - Простой setup (один Redis для всего)
    - ACK mechanism
    
    Минусы:
    - Требует Redis
    """
    
    def __init__(self, redis_url: str = "redis://localhost:6379"):
        self.redis_url = redis_url
        self.client: Optional[redis.Redis] = None
        self._running_consumers = []
        self._stats = {'published': 0, 'consumed': 0, 'errors': 0}
        
    async def connect(self):
        """Initialize Redis connection"""
        if not self.client:
            self.client = await redis.from_url(
                self.redis_url,
                decode_responses=True
            )
    
    async def publish(self, event: Event) -> None:
        """Publish to Redis stream"""
        await self.connect()
        
        # Stream name based on event type
        stream_key = f"events:{event.type}"
        
        # Serialize event
        event_data = {
            'id': event.id,
            'type': event.type,
            'data': json.dumps(event.data),
            'source': event.source,
            'tenant_id': event.tenant_id,
            'timestamp': event.timestamp.isoformat(),
            'priority': event.priority.value,
            'retry_count': event.retry_count
        }
        
        # Add to stream
        await self.client.xadd(stream_key, event_data)
        self._stats['published'] += 1
    
    async def subscribe(
        self,
        event_type: str,
        handler: EventHandler,
        consumer_group: Optional[str] = None
    ) -> str:
        """Subscribe with consumer group"""
        await self.connect()
        
        stream_key = f"events:{event_type}"
        group_name = consumer_group or 'default'
        
        # Create consumer group if doesn't exist
        try:
            await self.client.xgroup_create(
                stream_key,
                group_name,
                id='0',
                mkstream=True
            )
        except redis.ResponseError:
            pass  # Group already exists
        
        # Start consumer task
        consumer_id = f"consumer_{len(self._running_consumers)}"
        task = asyncio.create_task(
            self._consume_stream(stream_key, group_name, consumer_id, handler)
        )
        
        self._running_consumers.append(task)
        
        return f"{stream_key}:{group_name}:{consumer_id}"
    
    async def _consume_stream(
        self,
        stream_key: str,
        group_name: str,
        consumer_id: str,
        handler: EventHandler
    ):
        """Consume events from stream"""
        while True:
            try:
                # Read from stream
                messages = await self.client.xreadgroup(
                    group_name,
                    consumer_id,
                    {stream_key: '>'},
                    count=10,
                    block=1000
                )
                
                for stream, events in messages:
                    for event_id, event_data in events:
                        # Deserialize event
                        event = Event(
                            id=event_data['id'],
                            type=event_data['type'],
                            data=json.loads(event_data['data']),
                            source=event_data['source'],
                            tenant_id=event_data['tenant_id'],
                            timestamp=datetime.fromisoformat(event_data['timestamp']),
                            priority=EventPriority(int(event_data['priority'])),
                            retry_count=int(event_data['retry_count'])
                        )
                        
                        # Handle event
                        try:
                            await handler(event)
                            
                            # ACK message
                            await self.client.xack(stream_key, group_name, event_id)
                            self._stats['consumed'] += 1
                            
                        except Exception as e:
                            self._stats['errors'] += 1
                            print(f"Handler error: {e}")
                            
                            # Retry logic
                            if event.retry_count < event.max_retries:
                                event.retry_count += 1
                                await self.publish(event)
                            
            except asyncio.CancelledError:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Consumer error: {e}")
                await asyncio.sleep(5)
    
    async def unsubscribe(self, subscription_id: str) -> None:
        """Cancel consumer"""
        # Parse subscription_id
        # Cancel corresponding task
        pass
    
    async def close(self) -> None:
        """Cleanup"""
        for task in self._running_consumers:
            task.cancel()
        
        if self.client:
            await self.client.close()
    
    async def get_stats(self) -> dict:
        return self._stats.copy()

FACTORY: Pluggable Backends
python# eventbus/factory.py

from typing import Optional
from .core.interface import IEventBus
from .backends.memory import InMemoryEventBus
from .backends.redis_streams import RedisStreamEventBus

def create_eventbus(
    backend: str = 'memory',
    **config
) -> IEventBus:
    """
    Factory для создания EventBus
    
    Args:
        backend: 'memory', 'redis'
        config: backend-specific config
        
    Examples:
        # MVP/tests
        bus = create_eventbus('memory')
        
        # Production
        bus = create_eventbus('redis', redis_url='redis://...')
    """
    
    if backend == 'memory':
        return InMemoryEventBus()
    
    elif backend == 'redis':
        redis_url = config.get('redis_url', 'redis://localhost:6379')
        return RedisStreamEventBus(redis_url)
    
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown backend: {backend}")

USAGE: Как использовать везде
python# Где угодно в платформе

from eventbus import create_eventbus, Event, EventPriority

# Initialize (один раз при старте сервиса)
eventbus = create_eventbus('memory')  # или 'redis'

# Publish event
event = Event.create(
    event_type='workflow.stage_changed',
    data={
        'workflow_id': 'bia_001',
        'from_state': 'identify_processes',
        'to_state': 'analyze_dependencies'
    },
    source='workflow-engine',
    tenant_id='tenant_123',
    priority=EventPriority.NORMAL
)

await eventbus.publish(event)

# Subscribe to events
async def handle_stage_change(event: Event):
    print(f"Stage changed: {event.data}")
    
await eventbus.subscribe('workflow.stage_changed', handle_stage_change)

# Wildcard subscriptions
await eventbus.subscribe('workflow.*', handle_any_workflow_event)

Этот EventBus:

✅ Чистый интерфейс
✅ Два бэкенда (memory + Redis)
✅ Pluggable (легко добавить RabbitMQ)
✅ Type-safe события
✅ Retry logic
✅ Wildcard subscriptions
✅ Consumer groups (Redis)
✅ Zero vendor lock-in

🏛️ АРХИТЕКТУРНЫЕ ПАТТЕРНЫ
Есть три подхода, каждый правильный для своего контекста:
1. SHARED INFRASTRUCTURE (Централизованный)
infrastructure/
├── eventbus/           # ← Один центральный EventBus
│   ├── core/
│   │   ├── events.py
│   │   └── interface.py
│   ├── backends/
│   │   ├── memory.py
│   │   └── redis_streams.py
│   └── factory.py
│
├── database/
├── cache/
└── messaging/

# Все сервисы импортируют отсюда
from infrastructure.eventbus import create_eventbus
Плюсы:

✅ Single source of truth
✅ Легко обновить везде (один pip install)
✅ Переиспользование гарантировано

Минусы:

❌ Coupling между сервисами через инфраструктуру
❌ Сложнее version независимых сервисов

Когда использовать: Монолит или тесно связанные микросервисы

2. DISTRIBUTED (Каждый модуль свой)
intelligent-core/
├── eventbus/           # Своя копия EventBus
│   └── ...

workflow-intelligence/
├── eventbus/           # Своя копия EventBus
│   └── ...

community-intelligence/
├── eventbus/           # Своя копия EventBus
│   └── ...
Плюсы:

✅ Независимость сервисов
✅ Можно разные версии EventBus
✅ Нет shared dependencies

Минусы:

❌ Code duplication
❌ Сложнее обновлять (N мест)
❌ Риск расхождения версий

Когда использовать: Истинные микросервисы, разные команды

3. HYBRID (Shared + Local Adapters)
shared-lib/
└── eventbus-core/      # ← Core в shared package
    ├── events.py
    └── interface.py

infrastructure/
└── eventbus/           # ← Бэкенды централизованно
    └── backends/

intelligent-core/
├── adapters/
│   └── eventbus_adapter.py  # Адаптер под нужды модуля

workflow-intelligence/
├── adapters/
│   └── eventbus_adapter.py  # Другой адаптер
Плюсы:

✅ Переиспользование core
✅ Гибкость на уровне модулей
✅ Баланс coupling/independence

Минусы:

❌ Более сложная структура
❌ Нужна дисциплина команды

Когда использовать: Средние/большие системы с эволюцией

🔗 ИНТЕГРАЦИЯ: EventBus + State Machine
Вот как eventbus_publisher.py интегрируется с новым EventBus:
python# workflow_intelligence/integration/eventbus_publisher.py

from infrastructure.eventbus import create_eventbus, Event, EventPriority
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional

class WorkflowEventPublisher:
    """
    Адаптер между State Machine и EventBus
    
    State Machine генерирует события → Publisher отправляет в EventBus
    """
    
    def __init__(self, eventbus=None):
        # Используем centralized EventBus или создаём новый
        self.eventbus = eventbus or create_eventbus('memory')
        
    async def publish_state_changed(
        self,
        workflow_id: str,
        from_state: str,
        to_state: str,
        context: Dict[str, Any],
        tenant_id: str
    ):
        """Publish state transition event"""
        
        event = Event.create(
            event_type='workflow.state_changed',
            data={
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'from_state': from_state,
                'to_state': to_state,
                'context': context,
                'module': context.get('module', 'unknown')
            },
            source='workflow-engine',
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            priority=EventPriority.NORMAL
        )
        
        await self.eventbus.publish(event)
        
    async def publish_action_completed(
        self,
        workflow_id: str,
        action_type: str,
        action_data: Dict[str, Any],
        tenant_id: str
    ):
        """Publish action completion"""
        
        event = Event.create(
            event_type=f'workflow.action.{action_type}',
            data={
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'action': action_data
            },
            source='workflow-engine',
            tenant_id=tenant_id
        )
        
        await self.eventbus.publish(event)
        
    async def publish_validation_failed(
        self,
        workflow_id: str,
        errors: list,
        tenant_id: str
    ):
        """Publish validation failures"""
        
        event = Event.create(
            event_type='workflow.validation_failed',
            data={
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'errors': errors
            },
            source='workflow-engine',
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            priority=EventPriority.HIGH  # High priority для errors
        )
        
        await self.eventbus.publish(event)
        
    async def publish_milestone_reached(
        self,
        workflow_id: str,
        milestone: str,
        tenant_id: str
    ):
        """Publish milestone achievement"""
        
        event = Event.create(
            event_type='workflow.milestone_reached',
            data={
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'milestone': milestone
            },
            source='workflow-engine',
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            priority=EventPriority.HIGH
        )
        
        await self.eventbus.publish(event)

🔄 ОБНОВЛЕНИЕ STATE MACHINE
Обновим state_machine.py чтобы использовать EventPublisher:
python# workflow_intelligence/core/state_machine.py

from typing import Dict, Any, List, Optional, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from .events import WorkflowEvent, EventType  # Наши domain events
from ..integration.eventbus_publisher import WorkflowEventPublisher

class StateMachine:
    """
    Базовая машина состояний с event publishing
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self, 
        initial_state: str,
        tenant_id: str,
        event_publisher: Optional[WorkflowEventPublisher] = None
    ):
        self.current_state = State(name=initial_state, data={})
        self.tenant_id = tenant_id
        self.history: List[StateTransition] = []
        
        # Event publishing
        self.event_publisher = event_publisher or WorkflowEventPublisher()
        self._local_handlers: Dict[str, List[Callable]] = {}  # Local handlers
        
    async def transition_to(self, new_state: str, reason: Optional[str] = None):
        """
        Перейти в новое состояние с event publishing
        """
        
        old_state = self.current_state.name
        
        # Validate transition
        can_transition, error = self.can_transition_to(new_state)
        if not can_transition:
            # Publish failure event
            await self.event_publisher.publish_validation_failed(
                workflow_id=self.workflow_id,
                errors=[error],
                tenant_id=self.tenant_id
            )
            raise ValueError(f"Cannot transition: {error}")
        
        # Execute transition
        self.current_state = State(name=new_state, data=self.current_state.data.copy())
        
        # Record history
        transition = StateTransition(
            from_state=old_state,
            to_state=new_state,
            timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
            reason=reason
        )
        self.history.append(transition)
        
        # Publish to EventBus
        await self.event_publisher.publish_state_changed(
            workflow_id=self.workflow_id,
            from_state=old_state,
            to_state=new_state,
            context=self.get_context(),
            tenant_id=self.tenant_id
        )
        
        # Call local handlers (for immediate reactions)
        await self._emit_local('state_changed', {
            'from_state': old_state,
            'to_state': new_state
        })
        
    async def execute_action(self, action_type: str, action_data: Dict[str, Any]):
        """
        Execute action and publish event
        """
        
        # Execute action logic
        result = await self._execute_action_logic(action_type, action_data)
        
        # Publish completion
        await self.event_publisher.publish_action_completed(
            workflow_id=self.workflow_id,
            action_type=action_type,
            action_data=action_data,
            tenant_id=self.tenant_id
        )
        
        # Local handlers
        await self._emit_local(f'action_{action_type}', action_data)
        
        return result
        
    def on(self, event_type: str, handler: Callable):
        """
        Register local event handler
        
        Local handlers для immediate reactions (в том же процессе)
        EventBus для distributed reactions (другие сервисы)
        """
        if event_type not in self._local_handlers:
            self._local_handlers[event_type] = []
        self._local_handlers[event_type].append(handler)
        
    async def _emit_local(self, event_type: str, data: Dict[str, Any]):
        """Emit event to local handlers"""
        handlers = self._local_handlers.get(event_type, [])
        for handler in handlers:
            await handler(data)

🎯 ЗАЧЕМ ДВА УРОВНЯ СОБЫТИЙ?
1. Domain Events (events.py)
python# workflow_intelligence/core/events.py

from enum import Enum
from dataclasses import dataclass

class EventType(Enum):
    """Domain event types"""
    STATE_CHANGED = "state_changed"
    PROCESS_ADDED = "process_added"
    VALIDATION_FAILED = "validation_failed"
    MILESTONE_REACHED = "milestone_reached"

@dataclass
class WorkflowEvent:
    """Domain event (внутри Workflow Intelligence)"""
    type: EventType
    data: dict
    workflow_id: str
Используется: Внутри модуля для immediate reactions
2. Platform Events (через EventBus)
python# Infrastructure EventBus events

event = Event.create(
    event_type='workflow.state_changed',  # Platform-wide naming
    data={...},
    source='workflow-engine',
    tenant_id='...'
)
Используется: Между модулями для distributed reactions

📊 EVENT FLOW DIAGRAM
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                   STATE MACHINE                              │
│                                                               │
│  transition_to() ──┐                                         │
│                    │                                         │
│                    ├─→ Domain Event (events.py)             │
│                    │    └─→ Local Handlers (immediate)      │
│                    │                                         │
│                    └─→ EventPublisher                        │
│                         └─→ Platform Event (EventBus)       │
│                              └─→ Distributed Handlers       │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

EXAMPLE:

State Machine: transition_to('analyze_dependencies')
    │
    ├─→ LOCAL: Update progress bar (immediate)
    │
    └─→ EVENTBUS: 'workflow.state_changed'
         └─→ Case Collector (listens, records transition)
         └─→ AI Advisor (listens, prepares context)
         └─→ Analytics (listens, updates metrics)

🔌 DEPENDENCY INJECTION
Как инжектить EventBus в State Machine:
python# services/intelligent-core/main.py

from infrastructure.eventbus import create_eventbus
from workflow_intelligence.integration.eventbus_publisher import WorkflowEventPublisher
from workflow_intelligence.core.state_machine import StateMachine

# Create EventBus (once)
eventbus = create_eventbus('redis')  # или 'memory' для MVP

# Create publisher
event_publisher = WorkflowEventPublisher(eventbus)

# Inject into State Machine
state_machine = StateMachine(
    initial_state='identify_processes',
    tenant_id='tenant_123',
    event_publisher=event_publisher  # ← Injection
)

# Now all transitions publish to EventBus
await state_machine.transition_to('analyze_dependencies')
# → Automatically publishes 'workflow.state_changed' event

1. CASE COLLECTOR SUBSCRIBER
python# workflow_intelligence/case_library/event_subscriber.py

from infrastructure.eventbus import Event
from .collector import CaseCollector

class CaseCollectorSubscriber:
    """
    Слушает workflow события и собирает cases
    
    Подписки:
    - workflow.state_changed → записать переход
    - workflow.action.* → записать действие
    - workflow.milestone_reached → отметить milestone
    - workflow.completed → создать case
    """
    
    def __init__(self, case_collector: CaseCollector):
        self.collector = case_collector
        
    async def setup_subscriptions(self, eventbus):
        """Register all subscriptions"""
        
        # State transitions
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.state_changed',
            self.handle_state_changed
        )
        
        # All actions
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.action.*',  # Wildcard
            self.handle_action
        )
        
        # Milestones
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.milestone_reached',
            self.handle_milestone
        )
        
        # Completion
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.completed',
            self.handle_workflow_completed
        )
        
    async def handle_state_changed(self, event: Event):
        """Record state transition"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        from_state = event.data['from_state']
        to_state = event.data['to_state']
        
        # Record in case collector
        await self.collector.record_transition(
            workflow_id=workflow_id,
            from_state=from_state,
            to_state=to_state,
            timestamp=event.timestamp
        )
        
        print(f"📝 Case Collector: Recorded transition {from_state} → {to_state}")
        
    async def handle_action(self, event: Event):
        """Record action taken"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        action = event.data['action']
        
        await self.collector.record_action(
            workflow_id=workflow_id,
            action=action,
            timestamp=event.timestamp
        )
        
        print(f"📝 Case Collector: Recorded action {event.type}")
        
    async def handle_milestone(self, event: Event):
        """Mark milestone achievement"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        milestone = event.data['milestone']
        
        await self.collector.record_milestone(
            workflow_id=workflow_id,
            milestone=milestone,
            timestamp=event.timestamp
        )
        
        print(f"🎯 Case Collector: Milestone reached - {milestone}")
        
    async def handle_workflow_completed(self, event: Event):
        """Create final case when workflow completes"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        
        # Create case from collected data
        case = await self.collector.finalize_case(workflow_id)
        
        print(f"✅ Case Collector: Created case {case.case_id}")

2. AI ADVISOR SUBSCRIBER
python# ai_orchestrator/subscribers/workflow_subscriber.py

from infrastructure.eventbus import Event
from ..context.context_builder import ContextBuilder
from ..advisors.proactive_advisor import ProactiveAdvisor

class AIAdvisorSubscriber:
    """
    Слушает workflow события и готовит AI контекст
    
    Подписки:
    - workflow.state_changed → prepare context для новой стадии
    - workflow.validation_failed → suggest fixes
    - workflow.stuck → proactive help
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        context_builder: ContextBuilder,
        proactive_advisor: ProactiveAdvisor
    ):
        self.context_builder = context_builder
        self.advisor = proactive_advisor
        
    async def setup_subscriptions(self, eventbus):
        """Setup AI advisor subscriptions"""
        
        # Prepare context when state changes
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.state_changed',
            self.handle_state_changed
        )
        
        # Help when validation fails
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.validation_failed',
            self.handle_validation_failed
        )
        
        # Detect stuck workflows
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.time_in_state_exceeded',
            self.handle_stuck_workflow
        )
        
    async def handle_state_changed(self, event: Event):
        """Prepare AI context for new state"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        to_state = event.data['to_state']
        tenant_id = event.tenant_id
        
        # Pre-build context (cache it)
        context = await self.context_builder.build_for_state(
            workflow_id=workflow_id,
            state=to_state,
            tenant_id=tenant_id
        )
        
        # Cache context for quick AI responses
        await self.context_builder.cache_context(workflow_id, context)
        
        print(f"🤖 AI Advisor: Context prepared for {to_state}")
        
    async def handle_validation_failed(self, event: Event):
        """Proactively suggest fixes for validation errors"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        errors = event.data['errors']
        tenant_id = event.tenant_id
        
        # Generate fix suggestions
        suggestions = await self.advisor.suggest_fixes(
            workflow_id=workflow_id,
            errors=errors,
            tenant_id=tenant_id
        )
        
        # Publish suggestions back to EventBus
        from infrastructure.eventbus import Event, EventPriority
        
        suggestion_event = Event.create(
            event_type='ai.suggestions_ready',
            data={
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'suggestions': suggestions
            },
            source='ai-advisor',
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            priority=EventPriority.HIGH
        )
        
        await eventbus.publish(suggestion_event)
        
        print(f"💡 AI Advisor: Sent {len(suggestions)} fix suggestions")
        
    async def handle_stuck_workflow(self, event: Event):
        """Proactive help for stuck workflows"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        current_state = event.data['current_state']
        time_stuck = event.data['hours_in_state']
        tenant_id = event.tenant_id
        
        # Generate proactive advice
        advice = await self.advisor.generate_proactive_advice(
            workflow_id=workflow_id,
            stuck_state=current_state,
            time_stuck=time_stuck,
            tenant_id=tenant_id
        )
        
        # Send notification
        notification_event = Event.create(
            event_type='notification.send',
            data={
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'type': 'proactive_help',
                'message': advice
            },
            source='ai-advisor',
            tenant_id=tenant_id,
            priority=EventPriority.HIGH
        )
        
        await eventbus.publish(notification_event)
        
        print(f"🆘 AI Advisor: Sent help for stuck workflow ({time_stuck}h)")

3. ANALYTICS SUBSCRIBER
python# analytics/subscribers/workflow_subscriber.py

from infrastructure.eventbus import Event
from ..metrics.collector import MetricsCollector

class AnalyticsSubscriber:
    """
    Собирает метрики из workflow событий
    
    Подписки:
    - workflow.* → все события для analytics
    - ai.intervention → track AI usage
    """
    
    def __init__(self, metrics_collector: MetricsCollector):
        self.metrics = metrics_collector
        
    async def setup_subscriptions(self, eventbus):
        """Setup analytics subscriptions"""
        
        # Track all workflow events
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.*',
            self.handle_workflow_event
        )
        
        # Track AI interventions
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'ai.intervention',
            self.handle_ai_intervention
        )
        
        # Track completions
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.completed',
            self.handle_completion
        )
        
    async def handle_workflow_event(self, event: Event):
        """Record workflow metrics"""
        
        # Increment counter
        await self.metrics.increment(
            metric=f'workflow.events.{event.type}',
            tags={
                'tenant': event.tenant_id,
                'source': event.source
            }
        )
        
        # Time-series data
        await self.metrics.record_event(
            event_type=event.type,
            timestamp=event.timestamp,
            metadata=event.data
        )
        
    async def handle_ai_intervention(self, event: Event):
        """Track AI usage"""
        
        intervention_type = event.data.get('intervention_type')
        
        await self.metrics.increment(
            metric='ai.interventions',
            tags={
                'type': intervention_type,
                'tenant': event.tenant_id
            }
        )
        
        print(f"📊 Analytics: Recorded AI intervention - {intervention_type}")
        
    async def handle_completion(self, event: Event):
        """Calculate completion metrics"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        duration = event.data.get('duration_days')
        success = event.data.get('success')
        
        # Record completion
        await self.metrics.record_gauge(
            metric='workflow.duration_days',
            value=duration,
            tags={
                'module': event.data.get('module'),
                'success': success
            }
        )
        
        print(f"📊 Analytics: Workflow completed in {duration} days")

4. NOTIFICATION SUBSCRIBER
python# notifications/subscribers/workflow_subscriber.py

from infrastructure.eventbus import Event
from ..channels.email import EmailNotifier
from ..channels.websocket import WebSocketNotifier

class NotificationSubscriber:
    """
    Отправляет уведомления пользователям
    
    Подписки:
    - workflow.milestone_reached → celebrate!
    - workflow.validation_failed → alert user
    - ai.suggestions_ready → notify about help
    - notification.send → generic notifications
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        email_notifier: EmailNotifier,
        websocket_notifier: WebSocketNotifier
    ):
        self.email = email_notifier
        self.websocket = websocket_notifier
        
    async def setup_subscriptions(self, eventbus):
        """Setup notification subscriptions"""
        
        # Milestones
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.milestone_reached',
            self.handle_milestone
        )
        
        # Validation errors
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'workflow.validation_failed',
            self.handle_validation_failed
        )
        
        # AI suggestions
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'ai.suggestions_ready',
            self.handle_ai_suggestions
        )
        
        # Generic notifications
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            'notification.send',
            self.handle_notification
        )
        
    async def handle_milestone(self, event: Event):
        """Celebrate milestone"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        milestone = event.data['milestone']
        
        # Real-time notification (WebSocket)
        await self.websocket.send(
            tenant_id=event.tenant_id,
            message={
                'type': 'milestone',
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'milestone': milestone,
                'emoji': '🎉'
            }
        )
        
        # Email notification
        await self.email.send(
            to=await self._get_workflow_owner(workflow_id),
            subject=f"Milestone Reached: {milestone}",
            template='milestone_reached',
            data={'milestone': milestone}
        )
        
        print(f"🔔 Notification: Milestone reached - {milestone}")
        
    async def handle_validation_failed(self, event: Event):
        """Alert about validation errors"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        errors = event.data['errors']
        
        # Real-time alert
        await self.websocket.send(
            tenant_id=event.tenant_id,
            message={
                'type': 'validation_error',
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'errors': errors
            }
        )
        
        print(f"⚠️  Notification: Validation failed - {len(errors)} errors")
        
    async def handle_ai_suggestions(self, event: Event):
        """Notify about AI help"""
        
        workflow_id = event.data['workflow_id']
        suggestions = event.data['suggestions']
        
        await self.websocket.send(
            tenant_id=event.tenant_id,
            message={
                'type': 'ai_help',
                'workflow_id': workflow_id,
                'suggestions': suggestions,
                'badge': 'new'
            }
        )
        
        print(f"💡 Notification: AI suggestions ready")
        
    async def handle_notification(self, event: Event):
        """Generic notification handler"""
        
        notification_type = event.data.get('type')
        message = event.data.get('message')
        
        await self.websocket.send(
            tenant_id=event.tenant_id,
            message={
                'type': notification_type,
                'message': message
            }
        )

python# compliance/subscribers/audit_subscriber.py

from infrastructure.eventbus import Event
from ..audit.logger import AuditLogger

class AuditSubscriber:
    """
    Записывает все события для compliance
    
    Подписки:
    - *.* → все события (для полного audit trail)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, audit_logger: AuditLogger):
        self.logger = audit_logger
        
    async def setup_subscriptions(self, eventbus):
        """Subscribe to ALL events"""
        
        # Wildcard - catch everything
        await eventbus.subscribe(
            '*.*',  # All events
            self.handle_any_event
        )
        
    async def handle_any_event(self, event: Event):
        """Log every event for audit trail"""
        
        await self.logger.log_event(
            event_id=event.id,
            event_type=event.type,
            source=event.source,
            tenant_id=event.tenant_id,
            timestamp=event.timestamp,
            data=event.data,
            correlation_id=event.correlation_id
        )
        
        # Don't print (too noisy), but log to database

🎬 ORCHESTRATION: Как всё запускается
python# services/intelligent-core/main.py

from fastapi import FastAPI
from infrastructure.eventbus import create_eventbus

# Subscribers
from workflow_intelligence.case_library.event_subscriber import CaseCollectorSubscriber
from ai_orchestrator.subscribers.workflow_subscriber import AIAdvisorSubscriber
from analytics.subscribers.workflow_subscriber import AnalyticsSubscriber
from notifications.subscribers.workflow_subscriber import NotificationSubscriber
from compliance.subscribers.audit_subscriber import AuditSubscriber

# Services
from workflow_intelligence.case_library.collector import CaseCollector
from ai_orchestrator.context.context_builder import ContextBuilder
from ai_orchestrator.advisors.proactive_advisor import ProactiveAdvisor

app = FastAPI()

@app.on_event("startup")
async def startup():
    """Initialize EventBus and subscribers"""
    
    # 1. Create EventBus
    eventbus = create_eventbus('redis')
    await eventbus.connect()  # For Redis backend
    
    # 2. Initialize services
    case_collector = CaseCollector(db_session)
    context_builder = ContextBuilder(case_library, knowledge_graph)
    proactive_advisor = ProactiveAdvisor(llm_client)
    
    # 3. Create subscribers
    subscribers = [
        CaseCollectorSubscriber(case_collector),
        AIAdvisorSubscriber(context_builder, proactive_advisor),
        AnalyticsSubscriber(metrics_collector),
        NotificationSubscriber(email_notifier, websocket_notifier),
        AuditSubscriber(audit_logger)
    ]
    
    # 4. Setup subscriptions
    for subscriber in subscribers:
        await subscriber.setup_subscriptions(eventbus)
        
    print("✅ Event subscriptions configured")
    
    # 5. Store eventbus for dependency injection
    app.state.eventbus = eventbus

@app.on_event("shutdown")
async def shutdown():
    """Cleanup"""
    await app.state.eventbus.close()

📊 EVENT FLOW EXAMPLE
Когда пользователь добавляет процесс в BIA:
python# User action → State Machine
await workflow_engine.add_process({
    'name': 'Emergency Department',
    'tier': 'tier_1'
})
Что происходит:
1. State Machine
   └─→ Publishes: 'workflow.action.process_added'
   
2. EventBus distributes to subscribers:
   
   ├─→ Case Collector
   │   └─→ Records: process added at 14:23
   │
   ├─→ AI Advisor
   │   └─→ Updates context: now has 1 tier_1 process
   │   └─→ Checks: can suggest dependencies
   │
   ├─→ Analytics
   │   └─→ Increments: workflow.processes.added
   │   └─→ Records: tier_1 process added
   │
   ├─→ Notifications
   │   └─→ WebSocket: Real-time update to UI
   │   └─→ "Process added successfully ✅"
   │
   └─→ Audit Logger
       └─→ Logs: {user: X, action: add_process, timestamp: ...}
3 секунды спустя:
3. AI Advisor (proactive)
   └─→ Publishes: 'ai.suggestions_ready'
       data: {
         suggestions: [
           "Tier 1 processes typically depend on: staff, EMR, backup power",
           "Would you like help mapping dependencies?"
         ]
       }
       
4. EventBus distributes:
   └─→ Notifications
       └─→ WebSocket: Shows AI suggestion badge
       └─→ User sees: "💡 AI has suggestions for you"

🔥 ADVANCED: Consumer Groups (Load Balancing)
Для production с несколькими инстансами:
python# Instance 1
await eventbus.subscribe(
    'workflow.action.*',
    handler,
    consumer_group='case-collectors'  # ← Same group
)

# Instance 2
await eventbus.subscribe(
    'workflow.action.*',
    handler,
    consumer_group='case-collectors'  # ← Same group
)

# Result: Events distributed between instances (load balanced)

✅ РЕЗЮМЕ
Event-Driven Architecture:

State Machine публикует события
EventBus распределяет по подписчикам
Subscribers реагируют независимо:

Case Collector → history
AI Advisor → context + proactive help
Analytics → metrics
Notifications → real-time updates
Audit → compliance



Плюсы:

✅ Loose coupling (модули не знают друг о друге)
✅ Easy to add new subscribers
✅ Resilience (если один subscriber падает, другие работают)
✅ Scalability (consumer groups)

🧠 МОЯ АРХИТЕКТУРА AI/ML ПОДСИСТЕМЫ
1. AI EXPERTS - ГИБРИДНЫЙ ПОДХОД
Решение: Одна foundation model (Claude) + Specialized Agents с инструментами
Почему НЕ fine-tuning:

Дорого ($100k+ для quality BCM model)
Медленно (недели на подготовку данных)
Хрупко (нужно переобучать при изменении стандартов)
У вас нет 50,000+ labeled BCM conversations для обучения

Почему ДА специализированные агенты:

Claude Sonnet 4 уже знает ISO 22301 (в training data)
Специализация через system prompts + RAG context + tools
Быстрый iteration (меняешь prompt, не модель)
Cost-effective ($0.003/1k tokens vs $100k fine-tune)

Архитектура:
python# ai_experts/base/expert_agent.py

class ExpertAgent:
    """
    Base class для AI экспертов
    
    Специализация через:
    - System prompt (роль эксперта)
    - RAG context (релевантные знания)
    - Tools (специфичные возможности)
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        name: str,
        role_description: str,
        knowledge_sources: list,
        tools: list
    ):
        self.name = name
        self.role = role_description
        self.rag_pipeline = RAGPipeline(knowledge_sources)
        self.tools = {tool.name: tool for tool in tools}
        self.llm = AnthropicClient()  # Claude
        
    async def advise(self, query: str, context: dict) -> str:
        """
        Main advisory method
        
        Flow:
        1. Retrieve relevant knowledge (RAG)
        2. Build specialized prompt
        3. Generate response with tools
        """
        
        # 1. RAG retrieval
        relevant_knowledge = await self.rag_pipeline.retrieve(
            query=query,
            context=context,
            top_k=5
        )
        
        # 2. Build prompt
        prompt = self._build_prompt(
            query=query,
            context=context,
            knowledge=relevant_knowledge
        )
        
        # 3. Generate with tools
        response = await self.llm.generate(
            messages=[
                {"role": "system", "content": self._system_prompt()},
                {"role": "user", "content": prompt}
            ],
            tools=[t.to_anthropic_tool() for t in self.tools.values()],
            temperature=0.3  # Factual for experts
        )
        
        # 4. Execute tool calls if any
        if response.stop_reason == "tool_use":
            tool_results = await self._execute_tools(response.content)
            # Continue conversation with tool results
            final_response = await self._continue_with_tools(
                response, tool_results
            )
            return final_response
            
        return response.content[0].text
        
    def _system_prompt(self) -> str:
        """System prompt defining expert personality"""
        return f"""You are {self.name}, a {self.role}.

Your expertise:
- Deep knowledge of ISO 22301, BCI Good Practice Guidelines
- 15+ years BCM consulting experience
- Specialized in {self._specialization()}

Your style:
- Practical, actionable advice
- Reference standards by clause number
- Use examples from real cases
- Be encouraging but honest about challenges

Your limitations:
- You don't make decisions for users
- You suggest options with trade-offs
- You acknowledge uncertainty when appropriate
"""

    def _specialization(self) -> str:
        """Override in subclasses"""
        return "business continuity management"

python# ai_experts/specialists/bcm_advisor.py

from ..base.expert_agent import ExpertAgent
from ..tools.bia_tools import BIAAnalysisTool, DependencyMapperTool
from ..tools.case_library_tool import CaseSearchTool

class BCMAdvisor(ExpertAgent):
    """
    BCM Advisor - помогает с BIA, планированием, стратегией
    """
    
    def __init__(self, case_library, knowledge_graph):
        super().__init__(
            name="BCM Advisor",
            role_description="Business Continuity Management expert specializing in BIA and strategy development",
            knowledge_sources=[
                knowledge_graph,  # ISO standards
                case_library      # Real cases
            ],
            tools=[
                BIAAnalysisTool(),
                DependencyMapperTool(),
                CaseSearchTool(case_library)
            ]
        )
        
    def _specialization(self) -> str:
        return """business impact analysis, recovery strategies, and BCM planning.
        
You excel at:
- Identifying critical business processes
- Calculating recovery time objectives (RTOs)
- Designing recovery strategies
- Finding patterns in similar organizations' approaches"""

# ai_experts/specialists/compliance_auditor.py

class ComplianceAuditor(ExpertAgent):
    """
    Compliance Auditor - проверяет соответствие стандартам
    """
    
    def __init__(self, knowledge_graph):
        super().__init__(
            name="Compliance Auditor",

2. CASE LIBRARY SELF-LEARNING - SUPERVISED FEDERATED
Решение: Hybrid supervised + federated learning
LEARNING FLOW:

Organization completes workflow
    ↓
Case automatically collected (anonymized)
    ↓
Goes to REVIEW QUEUE
    ↓
    ├─→ Peer review (community experts)
    │   └─→ If approved → Public Case Library
    │
    └─→ Auto-patterns extraction (ML)
        ├─→ Success patterns identified
        ├─→ Common pitfalls extracted
        └─→ Suggested to RULE CANDIDATES
            ↓
        Human review (platform admins)
            ↓
        If approved → Active Rules/Recommendations
Почему гибрид:

Automatic collection - не требует усилий от пользователей
Supervised approval - quality control (избегаем bad practices)
Federated aggregation - каждая org учится от своих + анонимных чужих
Human-in-the-loop для rules - critical decisions need humans

python# ai_experts/learning/self_learning_engine.py

class SelfLearningEngine:
    """
    Движок самообучения платформы
    
    3 уровня обучения:
    1. Case accumulation (automatic)
    2. Pattern extraction (ML + human review)
    3. Rule generation (human approval required)
    """
    
    def __init__(
        self,
        case_library,
        pattern_extractor,
        rule_generator
    ):
        self.cases = case_library
        self.patterns = pattern_extractor
        self.rules = rule_generator
        
    async def learn_from_workflow_completion(
        self,
        workflow_case: WorkflowCase
    ):
        """
        Called when workflow completes
        
        Automatic learning без human intervention
        """
        
        # 1. Add to case library (anonymized)
        case_id = await self.cases.add_case(workflow_case)
        
        # 2. Extract patterns (ML)
        patterns = await self.patterns.extract(workflow_case)
        
        # 3. Update statistics
        await self._update_benchmarks(workflow_case)
        
        # 4. Check for rule candidates (threshold-based)
        if await self._should_suggest_rule(patterns):
            await self._create_rule_proposal(patterns)
            
        return {
            'case_id': case_id,
            'patterns_extracted': len(patterns),
            'benchmarks_updated': True
        }
        
    async def _should_suggest_rule(self, patterns: list) -> bool:
        """
        Предложить новое правило если паттерн повторяется
        
        Критерии:
        - Паттерн встречается в 10+ случаях
        - Success rate > 80%
        - Не противоречит существующим правилам
        """
        for pattern in patterns:
            frequency = await self.patterns.get_frequency(pattern)
            success_rate = await self.patterns.get_success_rate(pattern)
            
            if frequency >= 10 and success_rate > 0.8:
                conflicts = await self.rules.check_conflicts(pattern)
                if not conflicts:
                    return True
                    
        return False
        
    async def _create_rule_proposal(self, patterns: list):
        """
        Создать предложение нового правила для human review
        """
        
        proposal = await self.rules.generate_proposal(
            patterns=patterns,
            evidence=await self.patterns.get_supporting_cases(patterns)
        )
        
        # Queue for admin review
        await self.rules.submit_for_review(proposal)
        
        # Notify admins
        await notifications.send_admin_alert(
            type='new_rule_proposal',
            data=proposal
        )

3. ML MODELS - PREDICTION ПЕРВЫМ
Решение: Predictive models в приоритете (biggest ROI)
Почему prediction:

✅ Immediate value ("вот ваш roadmap на 6 месяцев")
✅ Reduces anxiety (пользователи знают что ждать)
✅ Enables planning (эксперты видят demand forecast)
✅ Differentiator (конкуренты не делают)

Потом anomaly detection и recommendations естественно вытекают из prediction data
python# ai_experts/ml/predictive_models.py

import numpy as np
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor, GradientBoostingClassifier
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

class WorkflowPredictor:
    """
    ML модель для предсказания workflow journey
    
    Предсказывает:
    - Длительность каждой стадии
    - Вероятность застревания
    - Момент когда понадобится помощь эксперта
    - Итоговое время до сертификации
    """
    
    def __init__(self, case_library):
        self.cases = case_library
        
        # Models
        self.duration_model = RandomForestRegressor(n_estimators=100)
        self.stuck_model = GradientBoostingClassifier()
        self.help_needed_model = GradientBoostingClassifier()
        
        # Trained flag
        self.is_trained = False
        
    async def train(self):
        """
        Train models на historical cases
        """
        
        # Get training data
        cases = await self.cases.get_completed_cases(min_count=50)
        
        if len(cases) < 50:
            raise ValueError("Need at least 50 cases to train")
            
        # Feature engineering
        X_duration, y_duration = self._prepare_duration_features(cases)
        X_stuck, y_stuck = self._prepare_stuck_features(cases)
        
        # Train models
        self.duration_model.fit(X_duration, y_duration)
        self.stuck_model.fit(X_stuck, y_stuck)
        
        self.is_trained = True
        
        # Calculate accuracy
        accuracy = {
            'duration_r2': self.duration_model.score(X_duration, y_duration),
            'stuck_accuracy': self.stuck_model.score(X_stuck, y_stuck)
        }
        
        return accuracy
        
    async def predict_journey(
        self,
        org_context: dict,
        current_state: str,
        current_progress: dict
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Predict полный journey от текущего состояния до completion
        
        Returns timeline с predictions для каждой стадии
        """
        
        if not self.is_trained:
            await self.train()
            
        # Features для текущей организации
        features = self._extract_features(org_context, current_state, current_progress)
        
        # Predict оставшиеся стадии
        remaining_stages = self._get_remaining_stages(current_state)
        
        predictions = []
        current_date = datetime.utcnow()
        
        for stage in remaining_stages:
            # Predict duration
            stage_features = self._add_stage_features(features, stage)
            predicted_days = self.duration_model.predict([stage_features])[0]
            
            # Predict stuck probability
            stuck_prob = self.stuck_model.predict_proba([stage_features])[0][1]
            
            # Predict help needed
            help_prob = self.help_needed_model.predict_proba([stage_features])[0][1]
            
            prediction = {
                'stage': stage,
                'estimated_duration_days': float(predicted_days),
                'estimated_start': current_date.isoformat(),
                'estimated_completion': (current_date + timedelta(days=predicted_days)).isoformat(),
                'stuck_risk': float(stuck_prob),
                'expert_help_probability': float(help_prob),
                'confidence': self._calculate_confidence(stage_features)
            }
            
            predictions.append(prediction)
            current_date += timedelta(days=predicted_days)
            
        return {
            'timeline': predictions,
            'total_estimated_days': sum(p['estimated_duration_days'] for p in predictions),
            'estimated_completion_date': current_date.isoformat(),
            'high_risk_stages': [p['stage'] for p in predictions if p['stuck_risk'] > 0.7]
        }
        
    def _prepare_duration_features(self, cases: list):
        """Extract features для duration prediction"""
        
        X = []
        y = []
        
        for case in cases:
            for stage in case.journey:
                features = [
                    # Org features
                    self._encode_industry(case.organization_context.industry),
                    self._encode_size(case.organization_context.size),
                    case.organization_context.maturity_level,
                    
                    # Stage features
                    self._encode_stage(stage.stage),
                    len(case.journey),  # Total stages
                    
                    # Historical features
                    case.metrics.ai_usage_count / len(case.journey),  # AI usage rate
                    len(stage.challenges),  # Complexity indicator
                ]
                
                X.append(features)
                y.append(stage.duration_hours / 24)  # Convert to days
                
        return np.array(X), np.array(y)
        
    def _prepare_stuck_features(self, cases: list):
        """Features для stuck prediction"""
        
        X = []
        y = []
        
        for case in cases:
            for stage in case.journey:
                features = [
                    self._encode_industry(case.organization_context.industry),
                    self._encode_size(case.organization_context.size),
                    self._encode_stage(stage.stage),
                    len(stage.challenges),
                    len(stage.ai_interventions),
                    stage.duration_hours / 24
                ]
                
                # Label: stuck if duration > 2x median
                median_duration = self._get_median_duration(stage.stage)
                is_stuck = stage.duration_hours > median_duration * 2
                
                X.append(features)
                y.append(1 if is_stuck else 0)
                
        return np.array(X), np.array(y)
        
    def _encode_industry(self, industry: str) -> int:
        """Simple encoding (expand with better approach)"""
        industries = ['healthcare', 'finance', 'manufacturing', 'technology', 'other']
        return industries.index(industry) if industry in industries else len(industries)
        
    def _calculate_confidence(self, features) -> float:
        """
        Calculate prediction confidence
        
        Based on:
        - How many similar cases in training data
        - Feature space density
        """
        # Simplified - в production использовать actual uncertainty estimation
        return 0.75  # Placeholder

4. RAG PIPELINE - ПРАКТИЧЕСКАЯ РЕАЛИЗАЦИЯ
python# ai_experts/rag/pipeline.py

from typing import List, Dict, Any
import openai
from infrastructure.database import get_db
from infrastructure.vector_db import get_vector_client

class RAGPipeline:
    """
    Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline
    
    Sources:
    1. Knowledge Graph (ISO standards, clauses)
    2. Case Library (successful cases, patterns)
    3. Community Annotations (practical interpretations)
    
    Process:
    1. Query → Embeddings
    2. Hybrid search (vector + keyword)
    3. Re-rank by relevance
    4. Return top-k chunks
    """
    
    def __init__(self, knowledge_sources: list):
        self.sources = knowledge_sources
        self.embedder = openai.Embedding
        self.vector_db = get_vector_client()
        
    async def retrieve(
        self,
        query: str,
        context: dict,
        top_k: int = 5
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Retrieve relevant knowledge chunks
        
        Hybrid approach:
        - Semantic search (vector similarity)
        - Filtered by context (industry, module)
        - Re-ranked by recency + relevance
        """
        
        # 1. Generate query embedding
        query_embedding = await self._embed(query)
        
        # 2. Search each source
        all_results = []
        
        for source in self.sources:
            results = await self._search_source(
                source=source,
                query_embedding=query_embedding,
                query_text=query,
                context=context,
                limit=top_k * 2  # Get more, will re-rank
            )
            all_results.extend(results)
            
        # 3. Re-rank by hybrid score
        ranked = self._rerank(all_results, query, context)
        
        # 4. Return top-k
        return ranked[:top_k]
        
    async def _search_source(
        self,
        source,
        query_embedding,
        query_text: str,
        context: dict,
        limit: int
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """Search specific source"""
        
        if source.type == 'knowledge_graph':
            return await self._search_knowledge_graph(
                query_text, context, limit
            )
            
        elif source.type == 'case_library':
            return await self._search_cases(
                query_embedding, context, limit
            )
            
        elif source.type == 'community_annotations':
            return await self._search_annotations(
                query_embedding, context, limit
            )
            
        return []
        
    async def _search_cases(
        self,
        query_embedding,
        context: dict,
        limit: int
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """Semantic search в case library"""
        
        # Vector similarity search
        results = await self.vector_db.query(
            vector=query_embedding,
            filter={
                'module': context.get('module'),
                'industry': context.get('industry'),
                'success': True  # Only successful cases
            },
            top_k=limit
        )
        
        return [
            {
                'source': 'case_library',
                'content': r['text'],
                'metadata': r['metadata'],
                'score': r['score']
            }
            for r in results
        ]
        
    def _rerank(
        self,
        results: List[Dict],
        query: str,
        context: dict
    ) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        Re-rank results по hybrid score
        
        Factors:
        - Vector similarity (from search)
        - Keyword match (BM25-like)
        - Recency (newer = better)
        - Source priority (ISO > cases > annotations)
        """
        
        for result in results:
            # Base score from vector search
            score = result.get('score', 0.5)
            
            # Keyword bonus
            if any(word.lower() in result['content'].lower() for word in query.split()):
                score += 0.1
                
            # Recency bonus (for cases)
            if result['source'] == 'case_library':
                days_old = (datetime.utcnow() - result['metadata']['created_at']).days
                recency_factor = max(0, 1 - days_old / 365)  # Decay over year
                score += recency_factor * 0.2
                
            # Source priority
            source_weights = {
                'knowledge_graph': 1.0,  # Official standards
                'case_library': 0.9,     # Real examples
                'community_annotations': 0.8
            }
            score *= source_weights.get(result['source'], 1.0)
            
            result['final_score'] = score
            
        # Sort by final score
        return sorted(results, key=lambda x: x['final_score'], reverse=True)
